Result: Under strong-to-weak distillation, math average is 9.7 points above vanilla OPD, and the smaller student surpasses the larger teacher. | CK |
| [2609.38282](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38282) | Improving OCR Faithfulness via Gated and Attenuated On-Policy Distillation | Multimodal understanding | External white-box | In joint post-training, gates and attenuates teacher supervision by the student's current task reward and local distribution: response groups with high reward turn off distillation, and forward KL is weighted by the student's probability of the teacher's Top-1 token. Result: CHAOS-Bench Micro Recall 59.92%, exceeding GRPO by 8.45 points and fixed-weight GRPO+OPD by 4.43 points. | C |
| [2609.38777](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38777) (withdrawn) | Distill the Visual Evidence, Not Just the Answer: Cross-World On-Policy Distillation for Vision-Language Models [code](https://github.com/baokou-fw2/CWAD) | Multimodal understanding | External white-box | Constructs two worlds sharing the question and scene but differing only in the key visual evidence, does OPD in both and distills the teacher's cross-world belief shift, so the student matches not just the answer but why the answer changes with the evidence. Result: Qwen3.5-4B beats the strongest baseline by 1.2 points on average, and on CWBench its CWPA is 22.4 points above DeepSeek-V4.1. | C |
| [2609.39120](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39120) | Is Better Teacher Supervision Enough? Unlocking Student-side Learning in Multimodal On-Policy Distillation [code](https://github.com/Sirilaw/S-OPD) | Multimodal understanding | External white-box | Points out that a bottleneck in multimodal OPD is still student perception: uses teacher-gated policy contrast between original and masked images to strengthen reliance on visual evidence, and aligns policies under noise perturbation to improve perceptual robustness. Result: Consistent improvements across eight benchmarks and different student scales, with up to +4.25 points on LogicVista, and it stacks with teacher-side methods. | CT |
| [2609.39275](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39275) | ReTaCo: Residual-Target Control for On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | Points out that EOPD's renormalized teacher top-k probabilities keep pushing the student's top-k mass toward 1, and instead uses a forward target that preserves the residual sign plus an unbiased single-sample reverse-KL estimator. Result: Proves the optimal top-k mass lies between m and m+beta(1-m) and increases with beta; outperforms EOPD on most math and code benchmarks. | C |
| [2609.39692](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39692) | GFD-OPD: Guidance-Folded On-Policy Distillation of Diffusion Models Across Scales | Image, video and audio generation | External white-box | Studies why large-teacher-to-small-student diffusion OPD fails, proposes Fixed-State KL to measure the teacher-student gap, and uses guidance folding to avoid CFG amplifying branch error. Result: The abstract states GFD beats existing baselines in both training efficiency and final performance and achieves the best results on all benchmarks. | C |
| [2609.39757](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39757) | Revisiting On-policy Adversarial Black-Box Distillation: Calibrating Groupwise Reward Geometry for Effective Advantage Construction [code](https://github.com/2018cx/GRGC) | General LLM | Black-box | Against the mismatch between the discriminator objective and GRPO's within-group relative advantage in black-box adversarial distillation, calibrates the discriminator reward distribution with Gaussian-quantile optimal transport, then applies group power modulation to widen advantage margins. Result: Across diverse teachers, student families and scales, and multiple training sets, beats GAD on both in- and out-of-distribution evaluations with negligible extra overhead. | C |
| [2610.02867](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02867) | TACD: Distilling Efficient Text-to-Motion Models via Terminal Amplification Control [code](https://vkgo.github.io/TACD/) | Image, video and audio generation | External white-box | Segment-wise on-policy flow distillation for text-to-motion: supervises clean-motion predictions along student-generated trajectories and ties teacher query points to the student step size to limit the weight near the endpoint. Result: An eight-step HY-Motion student cuts FID by 58% relative, with 7.7-11.9× end-to-end speedup and 3.8-6.7× less GPU memory. | C |
| [2610.03372](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03372) | SCAD: Structured Credit Assignment and Distillation for Long-Horizon Agents | Agents | External white-box | Splits long-horizon interaction into planning and bounded subtask execution: execution is distilled under teacher guidance within a local context, and planning refines credit with a cross-rollout subtask prefix tree. Result: Macro-average +4.48 points over the strongest training baseline on text tasks and +4.19 points on multimodal tasks. | C |
| [2610.04882](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04882) | ResOPD: Tail Residualization for Sparse On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | Unbiased reverse KL gradient estimation under a sparse teacher interface: groups the unobserved vocabulary into a coarse tail event whose gradient is computed exactly, and samples only the within-tail residual to greatly reduce variance. Result: At the same sparse transfer volume, it significantly reduces gradient variance, stabilizes online training and improves downstream performance. | CN |
| [2610.05373](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05373) | Towards Unbiased On-Policy Distillation for Block Diffusion Language Models | Diffusion language models | External white-box | OPD for block diffusion language models with large blocks: boundary-aware step filtering removes context misalignment, support-rebalanced confidence calibration suppresses overconfidence collapse, and rollout reuse lowers overhead. Result: More stable training and better performance on math and code benchmarks, with wall-clock training time roughly halved. | CN |
| [2610.05745](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05745) | Beyond In-Distribution Preservation: Recovering Generalization in Quantized VLAs via Vulnerability-Oriented Tuning [code](https://github.com/ruanruan-andy/PIVOT-Q) | Quantization and pruning | External white-box | Recovery for quantized VLAs: with the frozen full-precision policy as teacher, sparse OPD supervision is applied only at vulnerable states in the quantized student's rollouts, and a behavior anchor prevents needless changes. Result: Recovers consistently under seven LIBERO-Plus perturbations, using only 7.4% of the budget of all-state distillation while doing better. | - |
| [2610.06671](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06671) | Learning What to Imitate: Entropy-Aware Distribution Mixing | General LLM | External white-box | Interpolates per token between teacher and student distributions according to student predictive entropy, easing the conflict of being forced to imitate low-probability continuations at low-entropy, high-confidence positions; works for both offline trajectories and on-policy forward KL distillation. Result: Improves in-domain and out-of-domain math reasoning and better preserves general capabilities; the best entropy schedule varies with the source of the training data. | - |
| ★[2610.06804](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06804) | Sharpen Without Search: On-Policy Distillation of Sequence-Level Power Distribution [code](https://github.com/ArminAzizi98/OPPD) | General LLM | Own earlier checkpoint | Distills the sharpening effect of a sequence-level power distribution into a single generation: the model in training samples its own candidates, the frozen teacher's power distribution weights them, and the same weights drive a maximum-likelihood update. Result: On MATH500/GSM8K it gains 23.0 and 27.3 points over the untrained model, beats GRPO by 3.8 to 5.4 points at the same budget, and adds another 9.3 points on top of GRPO. | CN |
| [2610.07247](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07247) | Learning What to Distill: Bilevel Top-K Token Selection for Self-Distillation in Large Language Models | General LLM | Same model, privileged | Formulates Top-K token selection in on-policy self-distillation as bilevel optimization: the lower level uses a differentiable threshold relaxation to adaptively select positions as the student policy evolves, and the upper level distills only at the selected positions. Result: Best average performance on math reasoning benchmarks, at only a small extra compute cost. | - |
| [2610.08959](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08959) | GraphOPD: Graph-Augmented On-Policy Distillation for LLM Agents | Agents | External white-box | In multi-turn agent OPD, builds a step dependency graph from records of environment state changes, scores structural credit with the stationary distribution of a random walk, and fuses it with teacher-student divergence into a trajectory-relative supervision mask. Result: Across three scales and eleven baselines, up to +5.8pp on ALFWorld/WebShop/SearchQA; distilling only high-divergence steps is no better than picking steps at random. | T |
| [2610.09346](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09346) | OnlineQAT: On-Policy Distillation for Ultra-Low-Bit Large Language Models | Quantization and pruning | External white-box | Two-stage ultra-low-bit QAT: block-wise QAT first gives a usable low-bit initialization, then OPD recovery uses the frozen full-precision teacher's sampled reverse KL on prefixes generated by the quantized student itself. Result: On Qwen3-1.7B, W3A16 averages 57.28 and W2A16 averages 32.52, 2.90 and 0.44 points above ReasoningQAT. | - |
| [2610.11659](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11659) | DIAL-OPD: Learning More from Fewer Tokens in On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | Token selection: weights the reward magnitude by the logarithmic mean of teacher and student probabilities, suppressing low-low tokens to which both teacher and student assign very low probability yet which receive large log-ratio rewards. Result: Across 4 teacher-student pairs and 7 math benchmarks, keeping only 40% of tokens already beats all-token OPD, by up to +5.25 points on average. | NK |
| [2610.11989](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11989) | MetaOPD: Meta-Learned Token Weighting for On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | Jointly learns the student and a lightweight token-weighting network by bilevel optimization: the inner level updates the student with weighted OPD, and the outer level backpropagates the validation loss on reference solutions after a virtual update to optimize the weighting map. Result: On six math plus three OOD benchmarks, the 0.6B/1.7B students gain +1.99 and +2.25 Avg@8 points over OPD, respectively. | NK |
| [2610.13045](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13045) | Redirecting Teacher Supervision: Temporal-Response-Guided Latent Distillation for Video-Language Models | Multimodal understanding | External white-box | Internal supervision for OPD in video-language models: measures how representations respond to local temporal perturbations, adds the projection of the teacher correction onto the response-spanned space back to the original correction and restores the norm, thereby changing the supervision direction. Result: On four benchmarks including TempCompass and Video-MME, the 4B student's macro accuracy is 2.86 points above output-only OPD. | - |
| [2610.13264](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13264) | CV-OPD: Contrastive Vision On-policy Distillation | Multimodal understanding | Same model, privileged | Shows that OPD for VLMs takes visual shortcuts (still answering correctly with the image masked); builds a contrastive loss from the distribution gap between two teachers given positive and negative crops on the same student prefix, and gates on that gap to pick vision-relevant tokens. Result: Significantly mitigates shortcuts and improves visual reasoning on multiple fine-grained vision benchmarks (the abstract gives no specific numbers). | - |
| [2610.13272](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13272) | OWLET: On-Policy Weighted Learning via Evidence Transfer for Video Large Language Model Post-Training | Multimodal understanding | Same model, privileged | Video LLM: the same model serves as teacher with a privileged view (global plus question-relevant local windows), redistributes the response-level advantage by the token likelihood gap between the two views, and aligns spatiotemporal hidden states. | - |
| [2610.14022](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14022) | OPMD: On-Policy Mechanism Distillation Beyond Output Supervision | General LLM | External white-box | Pushes OPD from the output space to intermediate computation: it first builds a "student-usable" teacher by transforming teacher representations into intermediate states that the student's subsequent computation can digest, then internalizes these states along on-policy trajectories. Result: Achieves the highest average accuracy in all three teacher-student settings, and extends to multi-teacher distillation and cross-family SFT. | - |
| [2610.14572](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14572) | Reward-Aligned Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | Bilevel optimization weights each token's teacher supervision by its effect on the post-update reward; the weights are updated by differentiating through a temporary optimizer step followed by projected ascent, and can amplify, suppress or reverse the gradient. Result: On six competition math benchmarks it beats the strongest baseline by 6.5/2.5 points; on LiveCodeBench-v6, pass@1 reaches 54.69%/58.67%, 8.7/6.4 points higher. | - |
| [2610.14939](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14939) | VC-OPD: Visual Counterfactual On-Policy Distillation for Grounded Vision-Language Reasoning | Multimodal understanding | External white-box | Each student trajectory gets two teacher evaluations, on the original image and on a counterfactual image with the evidence destroyed: the original-image distribution is the target, and the difference between the two estimates each token's visual dependence for soft weighting. Result: Improves visual reasoning performance on multimodal reasoning benchmarks (the abstract gives no specific numbers). | - |
| [2610.17733](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17733) | CoCA: Criterion-Aware Credit Assignment for Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning | General LLM | Same model, privileged | In rubric-based RL, privileged rubrics are not treated as imitation targets: criterion-level credit is derived from group-relative outcomes, then apportioned to tokens according to the distribution shift caused by conditioning on the criterion. Result: Improves on standard rubric-based RL by 4.2% on average on medical and science benchmarks. | - |
| [2610.18459](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18459) | Teacher-Guided Tool Learning for Small Language Models via Bidirectional KL Constraints | Agents | External white-box | In tool learning for small models, injects token-level teacher feedback from reverse KL into the advantages of low-return trajectories, while using forward KL to continually preserve the teacher's demonstrated behavior. Result: On BFCL v4 and API Bank, all three student sizes surpass SFT, RL and distillation baselines. | - |
| [2610.18472](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18472) | Simplifying and Improving On-Policy Distillation for Few-Step Diffusion [code](https://zjwfufu.github.io/SMD-Page/) | Image, video and audio generation | External white-box | Uses purely regression-based on-policy velocity distillation for few-step diffusion: a Pivot residual proxy represents the teacher's velocity change within an interval, and weaker teacher predictions are repelled. Result: At 4-NFE it beats strong baselines and surpasses the multi-step teacher on several benchmarks, scaling up to 33 billion parameters. | - |
| [2610.18883](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18883) | Token Utility for On-Policy Distillation via Local Trust-Region Optimization | General LLM | External white-box | Derives token utility (in Fisher form) from the distillation objective itself, and on that basis gives FUW, a continuous mean-normalized token weighting, to replace discrete selection. Result: On seven math benchmarks with three Qwen3 teacher-student pairs, reverse KL gains 1.35-4.0 points and forward KL gains 1.08-2.71 points. | - |
| [2610.19572](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19572) | Divergence-Routed On-Policy Distillation for Language Models | General LLM | External white-box | Finds that positions with high JS divergence suffer from under-coverage of teacher modes, and adds forward KL supervision at these positions while keeping reverse KL at all positions. Result: Beats standard OPD and entropy routing on six math benchmarks, with a 37.9% relative reduction in severe under-coverage. | - |
| [2610.19601](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19601) | PA1-OPD: One-Step Prefix-Augmented On-Policy Distillation for Language Model Pretraining | General LLM | External white-box | In pretraining distillation, samples one extra student transition after a corpus prefix for the teacher to give feedback on, with data-anchored root nodes and differentiable tree attention to share prefix computation. Result: Cross-entropy beats KD at every checkpoint on C4/PG-19/Pile; selecting the 10% of roots with the highest surprisal adds a further 48.85% macro gain. | - |
| [2610.19820](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19820) | ActionOPD: Distilling Decisions, Not Tokens [code](https://github.com/thu-nics/ActionOPD) | Agents | Black-box | Samples semantic actions from both the student and a black-box teacher at states the student reaches, rewards semantic agreement between the actions, and performs on-policy distillation at the action level. Result: Qwen3-4B/8B gain 3.3-6.9 percentage points on average on four out-of-domain benchmarks, without needing a shared vocabulary or model family. | - |
| [2610.20664](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20664) | Information-Gain Calibrated On-Policy Distillation for Deep Search Agents | Agents | External white-box | Points out that deep-search OPD suffers from evidence-blind supervision, and uses post-retrieval information gain to calibrate both the allocation of the supervision budget and the direction of the teacher's preference over queries. Result: On six multimodal search benchmarks, 2B/4B students improve over OPD by 3.4 and 1.7 points on average, and the 4B student surpasses the 8B teacher. | - |
| [2610.21195](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21195) | Condition-Contrastive Extrapolation for On-Policy Distillation of Language Models | General LLM | External white-box | The same frozen teacher sees the full problem and the problem with one condition masked and contrasts them at the same positions; the bounded log-probability difference reweights the teacher distribution to suppress continuations that violate the premises. Result: On diagnostic states, the rate of preferring invalid continuations falls 27.0%→16.0%; avg@8 on six math benchmarks improves over OPD by 1.60 and 1.95 points. | - |
| [2610.21601](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21601) | Waypoint-OPD: Transferring RL-Induced Policy Shifts via Gated On-Policy Distillation [code](https://github.com/hhhbxloveai/WaypointOPD) | General LLM | External white-box | Uses the teacher's log-probability difference before and after RL as supervision, then takes an intermediate checkpoint to compare the direction of early and late shifts, and gates off that supervision when the directions are opposite. Result: Across six domain-student combinations, domain-average scores are all higher than those of the compared distillation methods. | - |
| [2610.21671](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21671) | Enhancing Multimodal RLVR with Pairwise Token Contrast and Adaptive Trajectory Confidence | Multimodal understanding | Same model, privileged | Credit refinement for multimodal RLVR: the teacher contrasts token by token across four views (correct/incorrect hint × with/without image), then scales the redistribution magnitude by the teacher's trajectory confidence. Result: Best average accuracy on three backbones; Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct ranks first on every evaluation. | - |
| [2610.23042](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23042) | GR-OPD: Group-Relative Sequence Credit for On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | Aggregates token-level teacher feedback into calibrated sequence-level credit: proves that broadcasting the summed feedback is an unbiased gradient of sequence-level reverse KL, discounts later-segment feedback, and calibrates with the group of rollouts for the same problem. Result: Beats vanilla OPD for all five teacher-student pairs on six math evaluations, by 10.9 and 11.5 points for the two pairs with matched thinking modes. | - |
| [2610.23181](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23181) | Tree-Aware Posterior Distillation for Long-Horizon Agentic Reinforcement Learning | Agents | Own earlier checkpoint | Treats each interaction turn as a tree node, branching from shared prefixes and propagating the final reward back as turn-level credit, which is then mapped to a closed-form bounded teacher distribution relative to the frozen behavior policy for self-distillation. Result: On Deep Research, Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking's average accuracy is 4.97 points above GRPO. | - |
| [2610.23306](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23306) | SimOPD: Relational Knowledge Transfer for On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | Uses a pairwise memory bank to turn the teacher's internal representations into a similarity distribution over student responses as supervision; the student matches it in its own representation space, avoiding cross-model projection. Result: Beats the strongest baseline by 2.6 and 3.1 points in same-architecture and cross-architecture settings, respectively. | - |
| [2610.25693](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25693) | ToolOPD: Credit-Aware On-Policy Distillation for Tool-Use Agents | Agents | Same model, privileged | Summarizes completed trajectories and outcomes into reusable hindsight skills, computes outcome-aware credit from how much the skills change the teacher's predicted probabilities of the student's action tokens, then weights the distillation loss with it and optimizes jointly with RL. | - |
| [2610.25973](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25973) | Correctness-Dependent Asymmetric Target Distributions for Self-Distillation Policy Optimization | General LLM | Same model, privileged | Builds asymmetric targets according to whether a rollout is correct: on correct ones it interpolates between the student and the self-teacher to preserve successful behavior, on incorrect ones it extrapolates beyond the self-teacher to strengthen correction. Result: Matches SDPO with on average 2.6× fewer training steps across Qwen3 scales. | - |
| [2610.28048](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28048) | On-Policy Transition Distillation | General LLM | Multi-teacher | Beyond the final-layer distribution, it also aligns teacher and student layer-wise log-probability increments on student rollouts, using the change of predictions along depth as an extra supervision signal. Result: Under three-domain distillation it recovers about 90% of the teacher-student capability gap, versus about 75% for OPD. | - |
| [2610.28194](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28194) | Adaptive Pullback: Reweighting Supervision at Penalty Jumps in On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | Observes that the penalty within a response jumps up suddenly and is accompanied by a drop in teacher confidence, and at each detected jump uniformly rescales the supervision coefficients of the following chunks using the previous chunk as reference. Result: Improves both math reasoning and code generation. | - |
| [2610.29378](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29378) | Right On Q: Recovering Reasoning under Quantization via Self-Generated Trajectories | Quantization and pruning | External white-box | Low-bit recovery: the quantized student generates its own responses, a full-precision teacher supervises on the student prefixes, and forward and reverse KL are weighted dynamically by the teacher-student probability ratio of each candidate. Result: Best across all benchmarks and all scales at 2-bit, with Qwen3-8B averaging 10.26 points above the strongest baseline. | - |
| [2610.30231](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30231) | Edit, Don't Imitate: Minimal-Edit On-Policy Distillation for Multimodal Reasoning | Multimodal understanding | External white-box | The teacher uses diagram descriptions and reference solutions visible only during training, and under a minimal-edit objective applies a correction loss only to tokens the teacher does not endorse, while correct rollouts go through task-reward RL. Result: At test time, without diagram descriptions, it matches or exceeds the base model given reference descriptions at inference. | - |
| [2610.30932](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30932) | UserArena: Benchmarking Interactive User Simulation for Long-Horizon Shopping Agents [code](https://github.com/RecGPT-Team/UserArena) | Other applications | Same model, privileged | Releases a real e-commerce long-horizon interaction benchmark and proposes OPTD, which uses trajectory-level intent to give dense teacher targets on the student's self-generated rollouts. Result: 64729 session trajectories; under this evaluation protocol OPTD has the strongest overall fidelity and comes closest to the policy effects of online A/B. | - |
| [2610.31563](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31563) | When to Follow the Teacher: Advantage-Based On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | Distinguishes what the teacher prefers from the actions that actually help the current student succeed, weighting teacher supervision by a counterfactual advantage with an importance-weighted unbiased estimator. Result: The objective can be estimated from standard student rollouts and final rewards alone; it beats distillation and RL baselines on math, code and Medqa. | - |
| [2610.37778](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37778) | Multi-Draft On-Policy Distillation for Diffusion Drafters | Speculative decoding | External white-box | On-policy distillation for parallel diffusion drafters: it samples several draft blocks for the same context and aggregates the verifier's full-vocabulary conditional distributions under different prefixes as the training target, mitigating prefix mismatch in parallel drafting. Result: Acceptance length exceeds the pretrained checkpoint and existing distillation methods on math, code and dialogue tasks. | - |

## Teacher-side improvements (59)

| arXiv | Title | Domain | Teacher | What it does | Lists |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [2608.01303](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01303) | AlphaG-OPD: Reliability-Gated Sibling Counterfactuals for On-Policy Distillation in Symbolic Alpha Factor Discovery | Other applications | - | Symbolic alpha factor discovery: compares grammar-valid sibling actions at intermediate AST states visited by the current policy and, after agreement and LCB gating, provides KL-bounded action supervision. Result: Strong cross-market results over multiple random seeds on CSI300/500/1000 and S&P 500 (no numbers given in the abstract). | C |
| [2608.01953](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01953) | Look Ahead Before You Distill: Future Trajectory Validation of Teacher Guidance for Agentic On-Policy Distillation [code](https://github.com/ChenChiShui/FutureBridge-OPD) | Agents | External white-box | Multi-turn agentic OPD: inserts a short teacher bridge at a high-disagreement state, then uses the subsequent student continuation to check whether the bridge really raises the density of positive distillation signals, and decides on that basis whether to use it. Result: Beats vanilla OPD by 16.6 points and TCOD by 7.6 points on average across three agentic benchmarks. | CTN |
| [2608.03673](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03673) | CausalOPD: First-Wrong-Step Supervision for Distilling Causal Chain Reasoning | Other applications | External white-box | Causal chain reasoning distillation: a knowledge-augmented teacher first supplies compliant trajectories, the teacher then locates the first wrong step in the student's own samples, short-horizon RL repairs it from the verified prefix, and a curriculum follows the causal stages. Result: Average path correctness is +23.4 points over sequence-level online process distillation across three domains, and the right-answer-wrong-reasoning rate goes 15.7%→4.4%. | C |
| [2608.05168](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05168) | Woodpecker Distillation: Weak Models Diagnose Reasoning Bugs in Strong Models | General LLM | Weak teacher | Contrasts successful and unsuccessful weak-model patches at the same strong-model reasoning prefix, builds a corrective teacher distribution and distills it back into the strong model. Result: Consistently better than baselines that directly imitate patches or repaired trajectories on math reasoning. | C |
| [2608.07068](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07068) | MemOPD: On-Policy Distillation through Memory State Alignment for Long-Horizon Agents [code](https://github.com/TPssp/MemOPD) | Agents | External white-box | Records the input and output of every call and restores the original token positions and causal visibility, so the teacher scores the states the student actually visited. Result: F1 +7.0% over persistent-history scoring, up to +416.2% over PPO, and 1.63× faster packing. | CTN |
| [2608.08726](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08726) | PAST: Privileged Adaptation from Complete Student Trajectories for On-Policy Self-Distillation | General LLM | Same model, privileged | Adapts the teacher using the student's complete trajectory and its success or failure as extra privileged information, while the student's distillation prefixes stay unchanged. Result: Macro-average Avg@12 on three math benchmarks improves by 5.6 percentage points over vanilla OPSD. | CTN |
| [2608.08768](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08768) | BOUND: Brief-Guided Corrective Preference Distillation at Search-Control Boundaries [code](https://github.com/RUCAIBox/BOUND) | Agents | External white-box | At the retrieval decision states the student reaches, the teacher judges which errors are correctable from a training-only search-state brief, builds state-matched preference pairs and distills them with DPO. Result: Leads on five of six benchmarks with re-run baselines, and is 5.6 EM above trajectory SFT on Bamboogle. | C |
| [2608.13391](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13391) | Context-Matched Distillation: Teacher Causality for Autoregressive Video Distillation | Image, video and audio generation | External white-box | Replaces the bidirectional teacher with a causal teacher and uses the student's already generated cached prefix as the scoring context, aligning DMD supervision with the student's information boundary at generation time. Result: Achieves the best overall performance among autoregressive methods on short- and long-video benchmarks, with clearly better camera-control following. | CTJ |
| [2608.14644](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14644) | DUET: Dual-Teacher On-Policy Distillation via Same-Weight Disagreement for Prohibition Compliance | Safety and privacy | Same model, privileged | Isolates the causal effect of a prohibition via the per-token disagreement between two same-weight teachers, one that sees the prohibition and one that does not; this is used to clean the signal and embed a DPO-style preference direction into OPD. Result: On Qwen 1.5B–8B, the compliance rate on violation cases is 72.3–85.2% while 88–93% of normal utility is retained. | CT |
| [2608.16320](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16320) | StreamOPD: A Post-Training Recipe with Spatio-Temporal Cue Gating for Streaming Video Understanding [code](https://unix-ai-lab.github.io/StreamOPD) | Multimodal understanding | External white-box | Post-training recipe for streaming video: OPD is done in thinking mode and the model is deployed in instruct mode; ST-CueGate aggregates teacher likelihood ratios with versus without spatio-temporal cues into group-relative scores to reweight OPD. Result: StreamingBench 77.9%→83.9%, 0.3 points from the 9B teacher; OVO-Bench +9.1. | TJ |
| [2608.18524](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18524) | DART-SD: Diamond-topology Aware Retrieval and Tuning for Self-Distillation of Multi-Turn Tool-Calling Agents | Agents | Same model, privileged | Models multi-turn tool calling as an interaction state-transition graph, retrieves successful recovery references at key topological breakpoints of autonomous rollouts, and computes the loss only on the generated recovery steps to protect valid reasoning prefixes. Result: Significantly outperforms conventional full-trajectory imitation baselines on complex multi-turn tool-calling benchmarks. | C |
| [2608.28647](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28647) | Self-Specialized Teachers for Domain Post-Training | General LLM | Own earlier checkpoint | First trains a copy of the base model into a domain teacher (key-token weighting + distribution alignment to the base), then distills the teacher distribution on the student's self-sampled prefixes, without external teachers or replay corpora. Result: With finance/medical/legal specialization, the average general-evaluation score rises by 4.8–5.0 points, consistently on Qwen3 and Gemma. | C |
| [2609.01567](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01567) | Selective Agent Guidance via Entropy: Learning Autonomous Policies from Imperfect VLM Teachers | Embodied | Black-box | Queries the VLM teacher and executes its suggested action only when the learner is uncertain, then distills teacher actions into a lightweight RL policy with environment-derived advantage weighting, so the VLM is no longer called at evaluation or deployment. Result: Beats unguided RL on sparse-reward visual reasoning and navigation tasks, and in some environments the student surpasses its VLM teacher. | C |
| [2609.02401](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02401) | CA-OPD: Confidence-Aware On-Policy Distillation for Structured Visual Prediction | Multimodal understanding | Multi-teacher | Uses teacher confidence to selectively correct unreliable student transitions and gradually hands rollout control back on a strict-to-loose schedule; corrected positions are supervised with cross-entropy and retained positions with the teacher's full distribution. Result: On Qwen3.5-0.8B, ScreenSpot-Pro +9.50 and OCRBench-v2 +6.72, with gains on all six benchmarks. | CTJ |
| [2609.02998](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02998) | Verify Before You Distill: Prompt-Level Teacher Gating for On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | Estimates the teacher's reliability on each prompt with a few verifier-scored teacher probes; prompts that pass use dense OPD, and the others switch to verifier-driven GRPO. Result: 4B/35B students beat plain OPD across all six single-domain settings; teacher-node GPU utilization rises from 9.8% to 78.9%. | CTNK |
| [2609.04773](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04773) | Persistent Teacher Anchoring for Tool-Using Agents | Agents | External white-box | Rollout construction for OPD of tool-using agents: block-level verification plus turn-level commit, where a tool call runs only after the teacher has verified the whole turn, with persistent lookahead filling idle capacity. Result: Under the same downstream RL budget, macro best@4 is 2.5 and 2.8 points above OPKD, and lookahead raises throughput by 24%. | C |
| [2609.20756](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20756) | OPTED: On-Policy Fine-Tuning for End-to-End Driving using a Render-Free Teacher [code](https://01dami23.github.io/opted/) | Embodied | External white-box | Closed-loop post-training for end-to-end driving decouples RL from policy training: RL first trains a render-free privileged teacher on vector inputs such as HD maps and bounding boxes, which then supervises a camera student in the loop. Result: Driving scores improve 1.6× for TransFuser and 9.5× for VaVAM, with about three orders of magnitude less simulator interaction than direct RL post-training. | C |
| [2609.22254](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22254) | Teacher Should Think Ahead: Adaptive Continuations for Reliable On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | Finds that a teacher continuing along the student's prefix shrinks its own uncertainty, argues through a variance-bias decomposition that continuations should be limited, and proposes teacher continuations at informative states with an adaptively chosen supervision horizon. Result: Consistently better than standard OPD on math reasoning and code generation across several model scales (the abstract gives no specific numbers). | CN |
| [2609.23435](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23435) | Tool-Augmented On-Policy Distillation for LLM Domain Adaptation in Sequence-Based Omics Tasks [code](https://github.com/SeedScientist/OmicsBench) | Other applications | Same model, privileged | First builds the multi-omics reasoning benchmark OmicsBench, then uses tool-augmented on-policy distillation to align sequence prediction with traceable chains of biological evidence. Result: Evidence groundedness strengthens consistently across five Qwen3.5 models from 0.8B to 27B, with prediction performance also improving on most tasks. | - |
| [2609.29518](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29518) | CataOPD: Catalytic On-Policy Distillation for Large Language Model Reasoning [code](https://github.com/QwenQKing/CataOPD) | General LLM | External white-box | An all-failure group first gets extra self-sampling to rescue itself, and only if it still fails does a teacher catalyze verifiable student trajectories, internalized with weights from the log-probability difference between guided and unguided. Result: Better than existing baselines, solves problems that were previously unsolvable, and generalizes better out of distribution when reasoning without catalysis. | CN |
| [2609.32303](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32303) | Train4Merge: RL or SFT---Which Yields Better Teachers for OPD-Based Model Merging? | General LLM | Multi-teacher | Compares, under the same initialization and the same task performance level, whether experts trained with RL or with SFT make better teachers for OPD-based merging, and checks whether this extends to multi-teacher OPD. Result: In all three domains, RL teachers make students stronger with higher capability recovery rates, along with smaller parameter shifts and higher teacher-student Top-K overlap. | C |
| [2609.32416](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32416) | RE-0: Verified Recursive Improvement of Embodied Code-as-Policy Agents through Local On-Policy Distillation | Embodied | External white-box | Asks a not fully reliable teacher for local corrections only on the student's failure histories and verifies their benefit in the environment, then runs OPD on the verified interventions weighted by their local gain. Result: On multiple Code-as-Policy tasks it improves both teacher-assisted execution and the standalone student, and generalizes to new robots and scenes. | C |
| [2609.32801](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32801) | PlanGuard: A Guardrail for Multi-Step Plan Safety in Embodied Agents | Safety and privacy | External white-box | A safety guardrail for multi-step plans: STAC-OPD transfers a strong teacher's token-level distribution along the student's online trajectories, and uses probability-routed sequence-level compensation to decide between the student and the teacher target. Result: PlanGuard-2B averages 87.15% ACC and 87.21% F1 across all test subsets. | J |
| [2609.34036](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34036) | UOPD: Uncertainty-Aware Intervention for On-Policy Distillation of Multi-Turn Agents [code](https://github.com/onepounchman/UOPD) | Agents | External white-box | Uses the teacher's low confidence in the student's action to pick high-uncertainty turns: low-uncertainty turns follow standard OPD, while high-uncertainty turns instead execute the teacher's action for the student to imitate, with an adaptive threshold controlling the intervention rate. Result: Beats OPD variants on ALFWorld/WebShop/Search, with up to a 15.8% relative gain on WebShop. | TX |
| [2609.35390](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35390) | Inductive Feedback for Mixed-Policy Distillation | General LLM | Same model, privileged | Treats verbal feedback as evidence for or against a token, uses a confirmation framework to build the target distribution within the student's trust region, and estimates a symmetric divergence with shared rollouts. Result: Beats conventional on-policy distillation recipes and recent contrastive variants on knowledge and agent benchmarks. | CT |
| [2609.35749](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35749) | Towards Communication-Efficient Social Intelligence in Language Agents | Agents | External white-box | Expression and strategy experts revise the student's actions; after probing with partner responses, references are chosen by goal support and token cost, and OPD runs on the student's own generated prefixes. Result: Highest Goal on SOTOPIA All and Hard, with far fewer target tokens than SFT+SDPO. | - |
| [2609.36246](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36246) | Learning from Teacher Continuations at Student States | General LLM | External white-box | In each round, OLIVE has the student generate a prefix and the teacher continue it autoregressively, applying cross-entropy only on teacher-generated tokens and needing no teacher token probabilities. Result: At equal cost it beats OPD with top-16 KL; on ScienceWorld it is 13% above offline SFT. | C |
| [2609.36546](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36546) | Interactive-Policy Distillation with Bidirectional Propose-and-Verify | General LLM | External white-box | To address teacher de-anchoring, the teacher and student swap roles under a bidirectional propose-and-verify state machine to produce mixed-source trajectories, supervision is applied separately by token source, and a fused inference engine is provided. Result: On Qwen3-30B-A3B→1.7B-Base, +3.28 mean@8 over OPD, surpassing OPD with about 1/4 of the samples. | C |
| [2609.36838](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36838) | On-Policy Visual Evidence Distillation [code](https://github.com/sylvain-wei/ReVuE) | Multimodal understanding | External white-box | Compares multiple student trajectories for the same query and diagnoses the first failure across the Acquire/Read/Ground stages, using the reflection as training-time context for the teacher and reweighting token losses by its influence. Result: Across 11 benchmarks spanning the Qwen2.5-VL and InternVL3.5 families, the weighted average score exceeds all OPD baselines. | C |
| [2609.37510](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37510) | From Dissonance to Orchestration: Teacher Intervention in On-Policy Distillation [code](https://github.com/yhao-wang/MAESTRO) | General LLM | External white-box | A controlled study of the trade-offs in teacher intervention depth and placement, proposing a local policy-disagreement score (teacher-weighted candidate coverage plus local distribution similarity, aggregated by reasoning paragraph) to adaptively decide when the teacher takes over and for how long. Result: Across eight math benchmarks, 0.6B/1.7B Qwen3 students achieve the highest macro-average, and training response length drops 67.3% relative to standard OPD. | C |
| [2609.37522](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37522) | Graph-Conditioned On-Policy Agent Distillation from Off-the-Shelf Teachers | Agents | External white-box | Uses a graph to index an off-the-shelf teacher's repeated executions by shared state, preserving complete successful and failed histories; retrieved references or historical alternatives combined with student hindsight score the student's original thought-action tokens. Result: ScienceWorld 24.70 to 48.78%, ALFWorld Unseen 53.36 to 85.26%, WebShop 29.10 to 37.65%. | C |
| [2609.38086](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38086) | VISTA: Internalizing Collective Visual Experience via On-Policy Distillation for Active Multimodal Agents | Multimodal understanding | Same model, privileged | Aggregates the visual observations from multiple rollouts of the same input into shared experience aligned with the interaction context, and has an experience-conditioned teacher evaluate the student's sampled prefixes, so even failed trajectories get experience-guided distillation signals. Result: Achieves the strongest average performance among active multimodal agents of comparable size, consistently beating same-backbone training baselines on fine-grained perception and general reasoning tasks. | - |
| [2609.38360](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38360) | On the Off-Policy Teacher in On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | Points out that the trajectories in OPD are off-policy for the teacher: the teacher continues worse on longer student prefixes, so it periodically adapts the teacher to student prefixes using verifiable-reward RL. Result: SCOUT improves the teacher's ability to continue from student prefixes and consistently improves OPD across multiple teacher-student configurations, model scales, and reasoning domains. | CNK |
| [2609.39687](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39687) | Better Supervision Is Nearby: Neighborhood On-Policy Self-Distillation | General LLM | Same model, privileged | Finds that local parameter perturbations of a privileged teacher yield complementary reference-aligned corrections; builds an expert pool offline, and online uses MaxPeak to set the anchor and quantile selection to pick the expert for forward KL. Result: On three math benchmarks (Average@12), beats OPSD by +2.75/+1.67/+1.94 points at 1.7B/4B/8B. | C |
| ★[2609.40285](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40285) | PivotOPD: Learning to Recover from Pivotal Mistakes in Multi-Turn Agents [code](https://research.nvidia.com/labs/lpr/pivotopd/) | Agents | External white-box | Finds multi-turn agent failures often stem from early recoverable pivotal mistakes; the teacher gives the correct action at the pivotal step via reverse KL for prevention, then recovery actions over the next few turns via forward KL. Result: On ALFWorld, a 1.7B student beats the strongest baseline by +5.5%, and SWE-Bench Verified resolve rate improves +3.2%. | C |
| [2610.02703](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02703) | Learning to Revise Reasoning with Segment-wise On-Policy Distillation [code](https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Seg-OPD) | General LLM | External white-box | Selects student reasoning segments by uncertainty and has the teacher rewrite them, training the student to prefer the teacher's rewrites while keeping per-token distillation, so it learns to revise reasoning. Result: Across models and tasks, reasoning accuracy improves by an average relative 5.22%, with a higher revision success rate than baselines. | C |
| [2610.02858](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02858) | Harness-Aware Distillation for Small Language Model Agents [code](https://github.com/moon4sake/harness-aware-distillation) | Agents | External white-box | Distills only the part of the teacher's capability that goes beyond the harness: contrasts the teacher's actions with and without harness information to build preference pairs, and discards pairs that contradict the records. Result: Beats same-harness on-policy distillation baselines on multiple long-horizon agent benchmarks, with fewer unproductive loops and easier error correction. | CH |
| [2610.04950](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04950) | How Should Teachers Be Prepared? RL on Student-Induced States for On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | Trains the teacher with RL before distillation: it continues from student prefixes and is rewarded by final-answer correctness, so it adapts to student states before giving per-token supervision. Result: With a 4B teacher teaching a 1.7B student, average accuracy is +8.28 points over standard OPD and +2.30 points over Relay-OPD. | CTN |
| [2610.05974](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05974) | Transfer-Stratified On-Policy Distillation for RL-Improved Reasoning Teachers | General LLM | External white-box | Studies which gains of an RL-strengthened teacher can pass to the student, and routes problems by joint teacher-student sampling success rate: acquisition problems get gated forward KL, consolidation problems get gated reverse KL, plus an entropy brake to keep coverage. Result: With a GRPO-strengthened teacher, it reaches the best macro-average accuracy, though pass@K stays unstable; the gains come from routing and token gating. | CTNX |
| ★[2610.10447](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10447) | A Good Self-Teacher Meets the Student Where They Are: Joint On-Policy Learning and Teaching | General LLM | Same model, privileged | Derives the necessary and sufficient condition for a privileged teacher's local distillation update to be a positive multiple of the student's reward gradient, and on that basis trains the same policy jointly as a KL-regularized teacher and as the distilled student. Result: Improves training efficiency and performance on math, code, tool use and terminal use, with further gains from adding the student reward. | N |
| [2610.10878](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10878) | Stochastic Teacher Intervention for Agentic On-Policy Distillation | Agents | External white-box | In multi-turn agent OPD, randomly decides whether to replace the student's action with the teacher's, with a probability mapped from the teacher-student KL gap, and corrects the sampling mismatch of mixed-policy trajectories with importance-weighted reverse KL. Result: On tool reasoning and long-horizon interaction tasks, it beats the strongest OPD baseline on every benchmark and student scale. | N |
| [2610.12367](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12367) | Which Skill to Distill? SGUID: Selecting a Compact Skill Bank for Model-Skill Co-Evolution | General LLM | Same model, privileged | Uses a skill-conditioned policy as the teacher for on-policy distillation, keeps only the few skills that consistently produce useful learning signals during training, and supports multi-round co-evolution of the model and the skill bank. Result: Fewer than 25% of retrieved skills are useful; distilling 6 selected skills matches the full bank, and Qwen3-8B gains 2 points after the second round. | NK |
| [2610.19302](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19302) | DTDD: Divergence-Triggered Dynamic Distillation for Reliable On-Policy Supervision | General LLM | External white-box | Uses segment-level teacher-student divergence to trigger teacher takeover: the student prefix gets the usual OPD supervision, and the student learns the teacher-generated recovery suffix by behavior cloning. Result: Peak gradient norm drops 8.6×; from base initialization it reaches 21.3% math accuracy with 2.6× fewer updates; ALFWorld improves by 4.8 points. | - |
| [2610.19397](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19397) | SERO: Learning from Executable Oracle Continuations for Long-Horizon Agentic Reinforcement Learning | Agents | External white-box | Restores the states reached by the student's current policy and lets a training-time oracle keep interacting in the real environment, weighting each oracle step by its reachability under the policy. Result: On ALFWorld, learning complete oracle continuations clearly beats supervising only the root action, and reachability weighting improves out-of-domain performance. | - |
| [2610.20572](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20572) | Recovering Reliable Supervision via Critique-Guided On-Policy Distillation [code](https://github.com/kzhao5/CriticOPD) | General LLM | External white-box | Points out that on wrong rollouts the teacher follows the student's errors, i.e. recoverability fails; instead, the teacher first critiques, then the student rewrites, and the teacher scores the rewrite. Result: Beats OPD with two Qwen3 students on six math benchmarks, with the largest gains on competition problems. | X |
| [2610.21427](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21427) | Learning from Interaction Experience via Localized Context Distillation | General LLM | Same model, privileged | First locates the key segments in a student trajectory that need revision; the teacher regenerates only these segments from the student prefix for the student to learn from, balancing informativeness and low exposure bias. Result: Consistently beats strong baselines on math, code and agentic tasks, with more stable training and smaller, sparser parameter updates. | - |
| [2610.25359](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25359) | CD-OPD: Contextual Digest for On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | Targets collaboration failures in which teacher takeover leads to a wrong answer: uses a teacher-generated dependency graph to organize the pre-takeover context into node summaries, and online meta-reweighting to align with task gradients. Result: Average accuracy on eight evaluations is 1.32–7.05 points above existing OPD, and the collaboration failure rate drops by 27.4%–56.4%. | - |
| [2610.25376](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25376) | TILDE: Teacher-in-the-Loop Distillation from Embodied Foundation Models [code](https://hku-sail.github.io/TILDE) | Embodied | External white-box | Distills the capabilities of embodied foundation models into a policy without action pretraining: performs on-policy distillation on student-induced states, and a robot reward model automatically triggers teacher takeover to collect recovery trajectories. Result: Improves up to 4.1 times over behavior cloning on three real-robot tasks, and collects recovery trajectories more than 2 times faster than human intervention. | - |
| ★[2610.27467](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27467) | Projected Distillation: On Learning to Solve Hard Problems from Close, Correct Targets | General LLM | External white-box | Treats on-policy-ness as a continuous spectrum, uses contrastive updates to minimally edit the student's failed attempts into correct targets, and refreshes these corrections online as the student evolves. Result: Theoretically the regret grows with the amount of editing; across five domains and two students it attains the best specialization-retention frontier, with retention dropping from 0.916 to 0.744. | - |
| [2610.27555](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27555) | Can Native Safety Travel? Reusing Safety Pretraining Across Models via On-Policy Distillation | Safety and privacy | Weak teacher | A standard pretrained student samples itself on safety prompts, a smaller safety-pretrained teacher gives token-level supervision, and cross-vocabulary alignment transfers safety across model families. Result: After further finetuning, the 1.7B student's ASR rises only 5.00% (11.58% for safety post-training), and its utility of 48.20% is above the teacher's 27.42%. | - |
| ★[2610.28586](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28586) | Capable Teacher, Myopic Scorer: Reward-Free Teacher Repair for Agentic On-Policy Distillation [code](https://tree-shu-zhao.github.io/r2s/) | Agents | External white-box | Argues that the problem with agentic OPD is not only compounding error but myopic per-token scoring: it mines disagreement states for the teacher to take over, finetunes the teacher scorer with reward-free verification, and then runs standard OPD. Result: Wins on all 12 combinations of three environments, two teachers and four students, averaging 12.8 success points above the respective strongest baselines. | - |
| [2610.30105](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30105) | Asymmetric On-Policy Distillation Learns In-Context Exploration | Embodied | External white-box | Under partial observability the student first gathers on-policy context on its own, a privileged expert gives action labels for that context, and the student thereby learns to explore before exploiting at test time. Result: Across 11 environments under a fixed exploration budget it gets about 2× the return with about 1/100 of the interactions, and +40% success rate on real-robot manipulation. | - |
| [2610.32802](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32802) | Frontier-OPD: Discovering the Edge of Agent Capability and Teaching Agents to Go Beyond | Agents | External white-box | Aggregates teacher-student disagreement on a shared-state trajectory graph to find the student's learning frontier; the teacher bridges only briefly at decision points the student can continue autonomously, and the bridges are filtered before entering the distillation data. Result: Autonomous performance exceeds the compared distillation baselines on ALFWorld, WebShop and ScienceWorld. | - |
| [2610.33011](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33011) | When Agents Collude: Weak-to-Strong On-Policy Distillation for Mitigating Strategic Deception in Multi-Agent Systems [code](https://agent-pact-deception.github.io/) | Safety and privacy | Weak teacher | After measuring collusive deception among multiple agents, proposes PACT: a strong student aligns, with reverse KL on its own sampled continuations, to the next-token distribution of a smaller honest teacher. Result: Multiple agents raise the misaligned behavior rate from 35.4% to 68.2%; PACT cuts the persistent deception rate from 80% to 3%. | - |
| [2610.33650](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33650) | TAD: Distilling Vision-Language-Action Policies from Teacher Trajectories Alone | Embodied | Black-box | Without querying teacher action scores, a discriminator compares the marginal distributions of joint-position features in teacher and student rollouts and turns this into an on-policy reward to train a VLA student. Result: On ManiSkill, OpenVLA-OFT rises from 26.2% to 76.6%, still 5.0 percentage points behind white-box OPD and with a larger interaction budget. | - |
| [2610.34005](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34005) | Steering the Teacher: Improving On-Policy Self-Distillation for Thinking Models | General LLM | Same model, privileged | Targets two failures of reference-conditioned teachers, reference hallucination and suppressed rechecking, by using activation steering to modify the teacher's hidden states before computing the distillation target, while the student itself is not steered. Result: Accuracy on AIME and HMMT exceeds vanilla OPSD, and the recheck rate and reference hallucination rate return to base model levels. | - |
| [2610.35690](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35690) | EvoRouter: Cost-Efficient Multi-Agent Routing via Insight-Guided On-Policy Distillation [code](https://github.com/CenYangtze/EvoRouter) | Agents | Same model, privileged | Experts distill performance-cost feedback into reusable routing insights; a privileged teacher then gives token-level supervision on the router's own planning trajectories, paired with cost-aware RL. Result: Averages 68.69% accuracy over 19 in-domain datasets, 7.23 percentage points above the strongest routing baseline, with inference compute cost down 50.95%. | - |
| [2610.38160](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38160) | ATI-OPD: Adaptive Teacher Intervention for Stable Long-Sequence On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | In long-response OPD a drifting student prefix weakens teacher feedback: the earliest high-disagreement segment is replaced with a short piece of teacher text for the student to continue from, and suffixes that keep diverging are excluded from the distillation loss. Result: On five math benchmarks and two teacher-student pairings, the student's standalone accuracy is on average above standard OPD. | - |
| ★[2610.40576](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40576) | PIVOT: Progressive Intervention via Online Teacher Correction [code](https://zhihaoyang30.github.io/PIVOT/) | General LLM | External white-box | Distinguishes trajectory-level intervention from token-level supervision and argues that neither alone is enough: teacher corrections are inserted into the student rollout, a token-level KL is applied to that segment, and the whole is combined with RLVR. Result: On hard logic games, avg@8 rises from 19.5% for the best baseline to 35.6%. | - |

## Rollouts, data and efficiency (28)

| arXiv | Title | Domain | Teacher | What it does | Lists |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [2608.10905](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10905) | ReOrder-OPD:Reliability-Aware Prompt Ordering for On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | Lifts teacher reliability to the prompt level: defines R as the probability that the teacher continues a student prefix to the correct answer, estimates it with a ROUGE proxy, and orders prompts by decreasing R. Result: Average R rises monotonically across ten proxy-score bins, with across-the-board gains on math and code for Qwen3 and Gemma4. | CT |
| [2608.18183](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18183) | Accelerating Visual On-Policy Distillation with Batched Speculative Jacobi Rollouts | Image, video and audio generation | External white-box | Turns speculative Jacobi decoding into a batched rollout backend: each image advances independently at its own pace, different positions are still verified in batch, and it switches between Full and Compact. Result: On LlamaGen it significantly reduces rollout and end-to-end training time while preserving the generation quality of the distilled student. | CTJ |
| [2609.14636](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14636) | Know When to Stop, Where to Restart: Accelerating Multi-Turn Agentic On-Policy Distillation | Agents | External white-box | Finds that teacher supervision concentrates in each turn's prefix and that the loss of teacher endorsement across turns is locked at the student's first wrong action, so it stops early adaptively by the teacher's cumulative log probability and restarts generation from the weakest correct turn using a prefix buffer. Result: On τ²-bench retail it matches full-trajectory OPD and beats the 30B teacher with a 3.73× speedup, and 4.51× in the cross-domain multi-teacher setting. | CN |
| [2609.24141](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24141) | CLOOPD: Closing the Learner Loop in On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | Decouples teacher scoring from student consumption: an adaptive α target is chosen inside a KL trust region, scored batches are frozen, and after each actor update a fresh forward pass measures realization and adds a further update. Result: Macro average goes 15.41 → 19.36, and Fixed3 reaches TOP-D level with 67.2% fewer teacher tokens. | CN |
| [2609.25891](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25891) | BAS-OPD: Budget-Aware Selective On-Policy Self-Distillation for Fine-Grained Multimodal Perception | Multimodal understanding | Same model, privileged | Teacher query budget allocation for fine-grained multimodal OPSD: the full batch of student generation is kept while only some rollouts query the privileged-view teacher, and three kinds of selection strategies are compared. Result: The abstract claims a large reduction in teacher supervision cost on fine-grained perception benchmarks while keeping strong performance, without specific numbers. | CJ |
| [2609.34250](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34250) | WAM-OPD: Sharpening World Action Models via On-Policy Distillation | Embodied | External white-box | OPD for robot world action models: replays a fixed trajectory pool with prefix weighting, and during replay the current policy generates new denoising paths for the teacher to supervise, so environment rollouts need not be collected repeatedly. Result: In simulation and on real robots, no extra environment interaction is needed during distillation; target tasks improve while the other tasks are preserved. | J |
| [2609.34838](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34838) | DivOPD: Spread Wide, Look Close for Asynchronous On-Policy Distillation of Multi-turn Agents [code](https://github.com/HanyangWang0418-oss/DivOPD) | Agents | External white-box | Learner-side batch selection for asynchronous multi-turn OPD: spreads the turn budget over more rollouts, prioritizes turns with large teacher-student divergence, and can also let the teacher take over briefly. Result: Average peak success rate over six settings goes 77.4→84.4, with 1.84/1.87× speedups in training tokens and GPU time. | C |
| [2609.36608](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36608) | Act First, Reason Later: Accelerating On-Policy Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents via Reference-Conditioned Inverse Dynamics | Agents | External white-box | In multi-turn agent OPD, the student first produces actions directly via reference-next-observation inverse dynamics to advance the environment, then asynchronously completes think-act responses for token-level teacher supervision. Result: Relative to naive OPD, wall-clock speedups of 2.3× on ALFWorld, 1.8× on WebShop, and 4.9× on ScienceWorld. | C |
| [2609.37170](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37170) | Interpolated Policy Distillation: A Controllable Continuum Between Off-Policy and On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | Views off-policy and on-policy distillation as the endpoints of a continuum, explicitly linearly interpolating the student and teacher next-token distributions token by token as the rollout policy, with exact speculative-decoding acceleration. Result: Across text and multimodal reasoning benchmarks, consistently outperforms SFT, OPD, the two-stage SFT then OPD, and heuristic segment-interleaving methods. | C |
| [2609.37500](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37500) | REVO: Rollout-Efficient Off-Policy Distillation via Variance-Guided Reuse [code](https://github.com/UNCSciML/REVO) | General LLM | External white-box | Reuses each student rollout for multi-step updates to save generation cost, corrects policy mismatch with prefix weighting and one-step resampling, and selects tokens by the variance of the teacher-student log-prob ratio. Result: With only 50 rollout iterations, matches or exceeds OPD baselines trained for 200 iterations (in-domain and cross-domain reasoning). | C |
| [2609.40055](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40055) | Less Data, Better Timing: Student-Curriculum Coupling for VLM On-Policy Distillation in Temporal Video Grounding | Multimodal understanding | External white-box | Distinguishes supervision trustworthiness from supervision necessity, defines the candidate supervision set with an Anchor-Frontier curriculum, and dynamically activates, suspends, or reactivates supervision based on the student's current competence. Result: Across three benchmarks, mean recall improves +5.1% relative to Video-OPD, with 60.0% fewer samples and 50.4% less training time. | C |
| [2610.02678](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02678) | Spend Teacher Tokens Where They Matter: Success-Referenced On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | Spends teacher compute only on prompts that have both successful and failed rollouts, selecting the failed trajectories whose hidden states sustainedly diverge from a successful reference for teacher supervision. Result: Across three teacher-student pairs and six math benchmarks, reaches comparable performance using only 3.46-5.02% of the teacher-input tokens. | C |
| [2610.05935](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05935) | ThunderSyncRL: Lossless Acceleration of Agentic Reinforcement Learning | Agents | External white-box | An agentic RL training system that computes gradients in a streaming way as soon as the inputs are fixed: GRPO by single-trajectory reward and OPD by teacher-scored actions of completed turns, updating in parallel during tool calls. Result: Proven equivalent to synchronous training updates; up to 1.9× faster on SWE-bench/Terminal Bench, and 2.47 points above asynchronous training at a fixed budget. | - |
| [2610.06105](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06105) | Flash-OPD: Fast On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | Replaces rollout length control with trajectory-level reliable-boundary verification: cached generation interleaves with teacher verification, and each trajectory is terminated independently by accumulated low teacher-student compatibility events. Result: 2.2 to 7.5× faster than standard OPD, with equal or better accuracy. | CN |
| [2610.07874](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07874) | On-Policy Distillation with Negative-Policy Rollouts [code](https://github.com/naver-ai/np-opd) | General LLM | External white-box | Keeps the form of the distillation reward unchanged and instead mixes a weaker negative policy into the rollout stage, so tokens the negative policy prefers stay exposed to teacher supervision, giving OPD an explicit negative signal. Result: Beats OPD across model scales, generation modes, reasoning domains and multiple OPD variants, and pushes the student away from the negative policy. | NK |
| [2610.09074](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09074) | TAP: Efficient Long-Horizon Agent Pruning via Trajectory-Anchored Recovery | Quantization and pruning | External white-box | Structured pruning for long-horizon agents: interaction is anchored on teacher trajectories, the student self-generates each turn's response while the frozen dense teacher supervises the prefix, and channels are iteratively reselected by the gradient of the recovery objective. Result: After pruning 60% of FFN channels, it retains 99.2% and 88.0% of the dense 7B model's success rate on ALFWorld and WebShop, respectively. | N |
| [2610.11332](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11332) | ReCal: Calibrating Structured Pruning for On-Policy Distillation Recovery | Quantization and pruning | External white-box | Pre-pruning calibration for OPD recovery: uses forward KL between the unpruned teacher and a pruned probe to find teacher-supported predictions that pruning breaks, and reweights calibration statistics so the pruning criterion keeps them. Result: Across multiple models and pruning methods, math reasoning after OPD improves by up to 16.7 percentage points (AIME). | NK |
| [2610.13238](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13238) | State-OPD: State-Grounded On-Policy Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents | Agents | External white-box | Anchors multi-turn agent OPD on interaction states: strips the student's earlier reasoning to recover reliable teacher continuations, and reallocates the sampling budget to states with high response entropy. Result: Highest macro average on three agent benchmarks; Qwen3-4B rises from 26.07% to 31.74%, surpassing the 32B teacher. | - |
| ★[2610.13481](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13481) | Branching On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | Selects decision points by student uncertainty and teacher-student disagreement, resamples student continuations from the shared prefix to form branches, and applies token-level teacher supervision to both the main trajectory and the branches; protocol-boundary repair is added to suppress repetitive generation. Result: Math avg@16 is 18.55 points above Vanilla OPD and ALFWorld success rate 13.8 points higher; shared-prefix reuse saves 19%/31% of tokens. | - |
| [2610.16352](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16352) | LoongSage: A Production-Grade, High-Efficiency Agentic RL Framework for Frontier LLMs with Validated Recipes | Agents | Multi-teacher | A fully asynchronous agentic RL framework of about 14K lines: a sliding window bounds trajectory staleness, weight synchronization is topology-aware, and a memory-efficient Teacher Manager natively supports multi-teacher OPD. Result: Across four full training runs, task reward rises steadily and the training-inference gap stays under control; the fully asynchronous pipeline eliminates synchronization bubbles. | - |
| [2610.21337](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21337) | Does On-Policy Distillation Need to Complete Every Rollout? | General LLM | External white-box | Randomly samples a stop position before generation to truncate student rollouts, then corrects the sampling bias with blockwise inverse probability weighting, and proves that the original OPD gradient sum is preserved in expectation. Result: Generated tokens drop by 25-48%, accuracy matches full OPD, and training is 1.3-2.2 times faster. | - |
| [2610.25679](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25679) | LOOP: Recovery-Aligned Iterative Layer Pruning via On-Policy Distillation | Quantization and pruning | External white-box | Alternates pruning and recovery: each round removes only one layer and runs short on-policy distillation, then reuses the on-policy trajectories and optimization signals from the recovery phase to assess each layer's contribution and pick the next layer. Result: Average accuracy consistently beats one-shot pruning baselines across model sizes and pruning rates. | - |
| [2610.25880](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25880) | Asynchronous On-Policy Self-Distillation | General LLM | Same model, privileged | Decouples rollout generation from learner updates for asynchronous OPSD, using staleness-aware policy control and token-level entropy weighting to mitigate outdated buffered trajectories. Result: Across three model sizes, throughput improves 1.96–2.25 times over synchronous OPSD, while average accuracy also rises. | - |
| [2610.27450](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27450) | Ask to Distill: Learning to Synthesize Data for On-Policy Distillation [code](https://github.com/GasolSun36/Ask_to_Distill) | General LLM | External white-box | Treats synthesized questions as distribution inducers, uses the disagreement on a frozen teacher-student pair together with the teacher probability mass as a reference-free question value reward, and trains a question generator that produces an OPD prompt pool. Result: Beats unoptimized prompts on math, code and agent tasks, and matches or exceeds OPD on real question banks. | - |
| [2610.27847](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27847) | SAPD: Learning from Agent Experience without Environment Interaction | Agents | External white-box | Splits offline multi-turn trajectories into single-step decision contexts; the student generates its own responses under the recorded history and receives teacher distillation, with relative step advantages stabilizing training and no environment interaction at any point. Result: On ALFWorld/WebShop/search QA, per-step training is at least 4.7× faster than online OPD, and it mitigates SFT forgetting. | - |
| [2610.34191](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34191) | Timing Matters Twice: When to Query and When to Skip in Flow-Matching On-Policy Distillation | Image, video and audio generation | Multi-teacher | Identifies the generation stage online from the correction magnitude of the predicted clean latent, and uses each teacher's stage preference to decide when to query supervision and when to skip the remaining rollout. Result: On SD3.5-M it executes only 5.56 of 16 rollout positions per batch, cutting training time by 41.3% versus DanceOPD while improving three metrics. | - |
| [2610.34272](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34272) | DoubleFree:Exploiting Exploration History for Rollout-Free Multi-Task LLMs Training | General LLM | Multi-teacher | Reuses the historical trajectories left over from the domain RL stage for rollout-free capability merging and precomputes teacher supervision to avoid keeping multiple teachers online, with an analysis of the conditions for the approximation. Result: On Qwen3-4B/8B and SmolLM3-3B, merging is 2.99-5.75× faster and GPU hours drop by 80.9-90.1%. | - |
| ★[2610.36477](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36477) | Proposing Better Rollouts for On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | Points out that always sampling from the student accumulates teacher-student mismatch, and derives from a trust-region objective a student-anchored, teacher-guided geometric mixture proposal policy for generating rollouts. Result: Consistently beats OPD on 16 math, knowledge and medical benchmarks; Qwen3-4B-Base gains +6.91 average math accuracy with generation length cut by 41%. | - |

Result: On VTCBench, the overall score of Qwen3-VL-8B goes 35.10→54.02, close to the 55.60 of native text input. | TJ |

Result: Consistently better than test-time and training-time self-evolution baselines on five math reasoning benchmarks and three model scales. | CTN |
| ★[2608.02948](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02948) | Rubrics as Privileged Information for Open-Ended Generation | General LLM | Same model, privileged | Uses rubrics as soft privileged information for OPSD, extending OPSD from verifiable tasks to open-ended generation, and finds that soft rubrics give a larger and more effective training signal on student trajectories than hard reference solutions. Result: On HealthBench it beats RaR RL by up to +0.10 and reference-solution privilege by +0.034~+0.079. | CTN |
| [2608.03223](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03223) | Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Self-Distilled Reward Shaping [code](https://github.com/gitrxh/ADRS-arxiv) | Agents | Same model, privileged | Uses a frozen policy snapshot to re-score the tokens of skill-free trajectories under the privileged condition of task-matched procedural skills, and folds the signal into native RL advantage construction after within-step normalization and return-associated gating. Result: Consistent gains on long-horizon tasks across three interactive benchmarks, still holding with a different RL backbone, less data and unseen tasks. | C |
| [2608.04007](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04007) | TurnSight: Turn-Level Hindsight Self-Distillation for Tool-Integrated Reasoning [code](https://github.com/quchangle1/TurnSight) | Agents | Same model, privileged | In tool-integrated reasoning, builds turn-level self-distillation supervision from execution-result hindsight: constructs several hindsight views with different lookahead horizons and selects reliable signals by cross-view directional agreement to modulate RL advantages. | CT |
| [2608.04788](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04788) | Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Observation-Calibrated Self-Distillation [code](https://github.com/yiy1x/OCSD) | Agents | Same model, privileged | Derives an observation residual from two structurally matched replay views, full and observation-ablated, to calibrate the token-level GRPO updates of OPSD. Result: Beats strong baselines on ALFWorld/WebShop/Search-QA at three Qwen3 scales. | CT |
| [2608.05131](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05131) | OPD-V: Visual On-Policy Self-Distillation with Modality Balance [code](https://github.com/aniri15/OPD-V) | Multimodal understanding | Same model, privileged | Treats modality balance itself as privileged information and selects self-distillation tokens by the logits margin between a zoom-in-image positive teacher and a mask-image negative teacher. Result: Stable gains and lower training cost across 6 benchmarks and 4 MLLM backbones. | CTNJ |
| [2608.05219](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05219) | When Privileged Guidance Misaligns: State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents [code](https://github.com/liujunzhuo/SMRC-SD) | Agents | Same model, privileged | Routes by the compatibility between the student's reached state and the reference solution, distills only on matched turns, and rewrites the reference with a reached-state summary. Result: Three model sizes beat full-path self-distillation on ALFWorld/WebShop/FTRL and transfer to BFCL and ToolHop. | C |
| [2608.05987](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05987) | AgentOPSD: Recursive Self-Distillation for Agentic Reinforcement Learning [code](https://github.com/ZethWang/AgentOPSD) | Agents | Same model, privileged | Aggregates token-level teacher-student log-probability gaps into turn-level evidence and recursively updates a Bayesian belief in log-odds space for turn-level credit assignment. Result: Qwen2.5-7B reaches 89.1% success on ALFWorld, better than GRPO and self-distillation baselines. | CTH |
| [2608.06057](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06057) | When History Lies: Evaluating and Improving Tool Use under Misleading Multi-Turn Histories | Agents | Same model, privileged | Builds a polluted-history benchmark and transfers the policy of an Oracle-state-conditioned teacher to the student through soft supervision on student-generated prefixes.
