| [2610.26856](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26856) | Reinforcement Learning with Visually Attributable On-Policy Self-Distillation | Multimodal understanding | Same model, privileged | Raises the problem of privileged correction contamination, filters token-level corrections by contrasting two teacher views with complete and with degraded evidence, and combines this with multimodal RLVR anchored on the verifier advantage. Result: Consistently beats GRPO and OPSD on six visual reasoning benchmarks and attains the best average performance. | - |
| [2610.26926](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26926) | AtomSD: Procedural Atom-Guided Self-Distillation for LLM Agents | Agents | Same model, privileged | Extracts Intent/When/Do/Avoid four-part procedural atoms from the model's own successful trajectories, conditions on the atoms to produce supervision distilled back into the policy, and optimizes it together with the task reward. Result: Average performance exceeds the strongest baseline by up to 9.31 points. | - |
| [2610.27495](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27495) | Self-Evolving Agents through Privileged Retrospective Distillation | Agents | Same model, privileged | The same model rescores its own trajectories using cross-task precedents, turn-level reflection and hindsight information as privileged context and distills this back into the policy, with the experience bank and the context policy co-evolving over multiple iterations. Result: On ALFWorld/ScienceWorld/SearchQA it improves over the base model by up to 59.7%, with the evolved context adding another 44.7%. | - |
| [2610.29167](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29167) | TeachBack: Learning to Teach via Bidirectional Preference Optimization for On-Policy Self-Distillation | General LLM | Same model, privileged | Shapes teacher behavior by controlling the length of the injected reference-solution suffix and the prompt style, with a bidirectional preference objective in which the teacher supervises the student and the student's behavior in turn adjusts how the teacher teaches. Result: Consistently outperforms existing OPSD methods and related baselines on math and logical reasoning tasks. | - |
| [2610.30880](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30880) | One Teacher, Many Views: Reducing Reference Sensitivity in On-Policy Self-Distillation | General LLM | Same model, privileged | Points out that conditioning on the full reference solution causes reference-induced target instability, and instead models the privileged context as a distribution over partial reference views, averaging in probability space before distilling. Result: Supervision is more consistent on math and science reasoning benchmarks, and it stably outperforms full-reference OPSD. | - |
| [2610.31438](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31438) | DIAL: Steerable Language Models with Distilled Instruction-Adherence Levels | General LLM | Same model, privileged | Uses classifier-free guidance on a frozen LLM to build teacher signals for different instruction-adherence levels, distilled on-policy into a lightweight text-conditioned steering adapter. Result: Balances concept expression and fluency well across several model families, with the concept score rising monotonically with the adherence level. | - |
| [2610.32770](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32770) | On-Policy Self-Training: Data-Free Self-Improvement in Large Language Models | General LLM | Same model, privileged | Turns on-policy self-distillation into self-training: from only a coarse topic description the model generates its own questions, answers and privileged reasoning traces and then self-distills. Result: Consistently self-improves on language benchmarks and visual question answering, while capability in domains not self-trained is preserved. | - |
| [2610.33667](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33667) | SWE-Ladder: Learning to Localize, Reproduce, and Patch via Progressive Privileged Self-Distillation [code](https://github.com/sekongger/SWE-Ladder) | Agents | Same model, privileged | Splits verified fixes into three kinds of stage evidence, localization, reproduction and patch, routes by tool call so a frozen teacher sees only the corresponding evidence, and distills stage by stage on top of GRPO. Result: On SWE-bench Verified, pass@1 rises from 9.6% to 16.4%, versus 10.4% for GRPO and 12.4% for full-patch OPSD. | - |
| [2610.34137](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34137) | Self-Evolution Reasoner: Continuous Optimization via Policy-Intrinsic Exploration and Exploitation [code](https://github.com/Eric8932/SEPO) | General LLM | Same model, privileged | Uses labels to induce the policy to produce correct reasoning itself and break the exploration bottleneck, then takes the model conditioned on that in-distribution reasoning as a self-teacher, distills it back into the policy, and iterates. Result: Best on science, tool calling and complex logical reasoning under equal training time or steps, with stable gains even on small models and hard tasks. | - |
| [2610.34415](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34415) | Spatial Audio Generation and Editing with Executable Plans and Self-Distilling Transfusion [code](https://wjm-george.github.io/AMBIT/) | Image, video and audio generation | Same model, privileged | A first-order Ambisonics spatial audio generation and editing framework that uses a target-conditioned teacher for joint self-distillation on student generation prefixes and on sampled states under the student's plan. Result: Beats the compared baselines on speech, music and sound effects in generated content and spatial quality, editing fidelity and preservation of unedited sources. | - |
| [2610.36082](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36082) | How Much Does the Hint Matter? Scope Conditions for Hindsight Self-Distillation in Financial Reasoning [code](https://github.com/johnnyhyu/fin-sd) | Other applications | Same model, privileged | The student locates its own first wrong step and writes a critique, truncates to before the error, and treats a frozen copy of itself given the hint as teacher for short-rollout distillation with reverse KL. Result: Replacing the critique with generic feedback reproduces the entire 3.03 percentage point gain; self-written hints work as well as hints from a model with 6× the parameters. | - |
| [2610.36959](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36959) | Live-OPSD: On-Policy Self-Distillation for Proactive Streaming Large Video Models | Multimodal understanding | Same model, privileged | A streaming assistant first generates a continuation from the context alone, then has the same model rescore that continuation with an evidence hint, and uses its distribution to supervise when and how the hint-free policy responds. Result: Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B averages 59.64 on LiveProBench, 5.26 points above the strongest continued-training SFT baseline. | - |
| [2610.37211](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37211) | Uncovering Shortcut Amplification in Video RLVR through the Lens of Perceptual Uncertainty Collapse | Multimodal understanding | Same model, privileged | Identifies perceptual uncertainty collapse in video RLVR, locates reasoning-sensitive tokens from correct and incorrect rollouts within a group, and reweights advantages by penalizing predictions that stay unchanged under a visually weakened view. Result: Experiments show that video reasoning performance and visual faithfulness improve together. | - |
| [2610.37281](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37281) | RPSD: Retrieval-Privileged On-Policy Self-Distillation with Optimal Transport | Other applications | Same model, privileged | In sequential recommendation, similar user trajectories retrieved by optimal transport serve as privileged evidence, a self-teacher rescores the student's on-policy responses, and the teacher-student token confidence gap turns the sequence-level advantage into token-level credit. Result: Achieves the best performance on several real-world datasets. | - |
| [2610.38154](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38154) | MASS: Multi-mask Selective Self-Distillation for Leakage-Resilient Reasoning | General LLM | Same model, privileged | Addressing a self-teacher that sees a single reference and so concentrates its predictions on one reasoning path, it approximates the reference marginal with multiple independent masked views of the same verified solution and selectively transfers reliable token-level corrections. Result: Stably outperforms strong self-distillation baselines across several math reasoning benchmarks and model families. | - |
| [2610.38391](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38391) | SCOPE: Selective Critical-Step On-Policy Self-Distillation for Agentic Reinforcement Learning | Agents | Same model, privileged | In agentic RL the policy analyzes its own trajectories to produce step-level hindsight skills, distills their guidance only at the selected wrong steps, and stabilizes the auxiliary update with a bounded signed teacher-student coefficient. Result: On Qwen2.5-3B it reaches 97.7% on ALFWorld and 85.2% on WebShop, beating SEED by 5.9 and 6.3 points. | - |
| [2610.38561](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38561) | Persona Learning from Feedback for Personalized AI Assistants | Other applications | Same model, privileged | A personalized assistant with one LoRA per persona: a frozen self-teacher, conditioned on the spec and on response feedback, gives token-level supervision on the adapter's self-generated prefixes, with a gate that corrects only responses needing improvement. Result: Model-judged preference alignment on single-turn personalization and multi-turn tutoring beats persona prompting and persona distillation. | - |
| [2610.38637](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38637) | On-Policy Self-Distillation with Teacher Evolution | General LLM | Same model, privileged | Attributes stalled OPSD gains to weak self-teaching ability: the self-teacher rolls out on its own and is optimized on the teacher side with rewards for correctness and context independence, after which unreliable samples are down-weighted by reliability. Result: Stably outperforms representative token-level and sequence-level methods across datasets and scales. | - |
| ★[2610.38681](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38681) | Weaken to Strengthen: Weakening-Induced On-Policy Self-Distillation for Large Reasoning Models [code](https://github.com/jet-ai-projects/WISD) | General LLM | Weak teacher | Self-distillation without privileged information: it builds a weaker copy of the model by low-bit quantization or magnitude pruning and uses the contrast between the original model and the weak copy as the on-policy training signal. Result: Quantization and pruning give average gains of 4.0 and 5.5 points; Qwen3.5-4B goes from 57.7% to 70.5% on HMMT 2025. | - |
| [2610.38732](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38732) | Adversarial Self-Distillation | Safety and privacy | Same model, privileged | Adversarially robust self-distillation: the same model under a clean prompt (or with an extra refusal instruction) serves as teacher, distilling refusal behavior on the prefixes the attacked student reaches by its own sampling, including compliant openings. Result: Attack success rate is below the strongest defense baseline with very little loss in multiple-choice accuracy, and refusal is still recovered after a compliant opening. | - |
| [2610.40115](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40115) | SelfThinker: Self-Evolving Reasoning via Reliability-Verified Intrinsic Visual Thoughts [code](https://github.com/zhangquanchen/SelfThinker) | Multimodal understanding | Same model, privileged | A unified model samples auxiliary images as visual thoughts and filters them by scoring with a frozen understanding pathway under conditional pointwise mutual information; the high-value visual thoughts both refine the generator and serve as privileged teacher context for on-policy self-distillation. Result: ThinkMorph-7B improves its average over seven benchmarks from 52.7 to 64.0 (+11.3 points). | - |
| [2610.40574](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40574) | Every Token Needs Its Own Teacher: Multi-teacher Adaptive On-policy Self-distillation [code](https://github.com/xingzhejun/MTA-OPSD) | General LLM | Multi-teacher | Observes that different tokens within one response prefer different teachers, builds a pool of candidate self-teachers with different reference exposure, and lets each token pick its teacher adaptively through majority voting and KL selection. Result: On Qwen3 math reasoning it gains up to +5.9 on average over the base model and stably outperforms existing OPSD methods. | - |
| [2610.41307](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41307) | Erase the Injected Region: Multimodal Prompt-Injection Defense with On-Policy Self-Distillation | Safety and privacy | Same model, privileged | Multimodal prompt-injection defense: erasing the injected region in place forms a reference view, a frozen base model reads that view, and the student that reads the attacked media gets full-distribution token-level supervision on its own rollouts. Result: Under attack the 3B audio model reaches 60.1% accuracy (33.1% for ARGUS), and injection reliance drops from 6.26 to 0.0095. | - |

## Multi-teacher OPD (MOPD) (42)

| arXiv | Title | Domain | Teacher | What it does | Lists |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [2608.03092](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03092) | SMOPD: Multi-Reward Reinforcement Learning via Specialize-and-Merge Online Policy Distillation | General LLM | Multi-teacher | For multi-reward RL, first trains several single-reward specialized teachers under reward-priority configurations, then merges their capabilities into one student with online policy distillation, keeping sparse reward signals from being drowned out by dense ones. Result: Beats GDPO on 1.5B/3B/7B backbones under both complementary and conflicting reward settings. | CTN |
| [2608.03610](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03610) | Language-Specialized Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for Multilingual LLM-Based ASR | Multimodal understanding | Multi-teacher | Multilingual LLM-ASR: first trains language-specialized teachers with RL, then integrates them into a unified student through language routing and token-level multi-teacher on-policy distillation. Result: Beats RL baselines on Mandarin, dialect, Cantonese and English benchmarks, and surpasses the best RL teacher on almost all of them. | TD |
| [2608.04349](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04349) | Poly-OPD: Heterogeneous Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for Capability-Selectable Flow Models | Image, video and audio generation | Multi-teacher | Uses a pixel bridge to merge several text-to-image teachers with incompatible latent spaces into one student: generated images are re-encoded and refined by a teacher, then aligned in frozen DINOv2 space. Result: FLUX.1-dev and Z-Image are distilled into a 2.5B student, raising GenEval 67.3→73.3 and beating both larger teachers. | CTJD |
| [2608.06490](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06490) | InsertFuse: A Unified Framework for Multi-Category Reference-Guided Image Insertion [code](https://insertfuse.github.io/) | Image, video and audio generation | Multi-teacher | First trains per-category insertion experts, then queries the matching expert at student-visited states for OPD, merging the multi-category capabilities into one unified student. Result: Reaches SOTA on most metrics on the AnyInsertion benchmark and an in-house multi-category test set. | TJ |
| [2608.07620](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07620) | FlowErase-OPD: Multi-Concept Erasure via Anchored On-Policy Distillation in Flow Matching Models | Safety and privacy | Multi-teacher | Distills several single-concept erasure models into one unified LoRA through anchored multi-teacher OPD, adaptively scheduling the weights of the erasure teachers and the retention teachers. Result: Reaches SOTA on the trade-off between erasure effect, image quality and semantic consistency for multi-concept erasure, and resists adversarial attacks. | CTJ |
| [2608.09440](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09440) | MetaStrategy: Generative Ranking with Executable LLM Strategies | Other applications | Multi-teacher | Industrial re-ranking: an LLM generates executable ranking-strategy JSON, and reward-augmented on-policy distillation routed by an Evaluator merges several 4B teachers into a 0.8B student. Result: A seven-day A/B test on the Taobao homepage: click PV +2.11%, IPV +3.12%, transaction value +2.83%. | - |
| [2608.19098](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19098) | Open-MOPD: Diagnosing and Fixing Capability Imbalance in Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation [code](https://bytedtsinghua-sia.github.io/Open-MOPD/) | General LLM | Multi-teacher | On a controlled benchmark, finds that the integration gap in multi-teacher OPD stems from token-budget mismatch rather than gradient conflict, and proposes share balancing, gap-aware budget allocation and student reward refresh. Result: On SmolLM3-3B-Base the recoverable headroom rises from 35.6% to 83.4%, and the full-pipeline recipe is open-sourced. | CTND |
| [2608.24987](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24987) | D³-MOPD: Dynamic Domain ScheDuling for Efficient Multi-Teacher Distillation | General LLM | Multi-teacher | Reuses the per-domain reverse KL signals already produced during training; an out-of-process observer estimates the remaining headroom and improvement rate and adjusts the domain sampling ratios of multi-teacher OPD online, with zero extra overhead. Result: A Qwen3.6-35B-A3B student closes 97% of the average gap to its four teachers (vanilla: 63%), with about 3× fewer rollout steps. | CND |
| [2608.26735](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26735) | Recovering General Capabilities via Uncertainty-Calibrated Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | Multi-teacher | Recovering general capabilities with multi-teacher OPD: high-temperature exploration paired with normal-temperature anchors picks trajectories with stronger positive advantage, then centered log-likelihood uses teacher uncertainty to filter out token updates the teacher does not endorse. Result: For role-play and medical specialization, the average general-capability score is 4.48% and 7.86% higher than standard MOPD, respectively. | ND |
| [2609.02548](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02548) | Learn from Whoever Is Right: Answer-Verified Multi-Teacher Distillation for Multi-Domain LLMs [code](https://github.com/hexixiang/MT-SDPO) | General LLM | Multi-teacher | Multi-teacher on-policy distillation that picks teachers per sample rather than per domain: correct rollouts within the group serve as self-anchors, teacher answers must pass verification before supervising, and both are merged into the privileged context of an EMA self-teacher. Result: Qwen3-8B gains 14.79 points on its weakest domain and the domain gap shrinks by 74.7%, covering five students from three families. | CND |
| [2609.07137](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07137) | Flow3D-OPD: Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for 3D Geometry Generation with Flow-Matching Diffusion Transformer | Image, video and audio generation | Multi-teacher | Two-stage post-training for image-to-3D: expert teachers are first cultivated with an agentic verifier through DPO, then multi-teacher OPD transfers them into a unified student with hard-task routed sampling to reduce gradient interference. Result: Consistent improvements on every geometry quality dimension, with an average metric above all teacher models. | CJD |
| [2609.09300](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09300) | Video-MOPD: Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for Video Understanding [code](https://huggingface.co/LandH/Video-MOPD-8B) | Multimodal understanding | Multi-teacher | Experts are first trained with RL in the three domains of temporal grounding, general understanding and video STEM, then multi-teacher OPD supervises student trajectories with routed teacher feedback plus reliability-aware sample selection. Result: Video-MOPD-8B reaches SOTA at its scale on general video understanding, temporal grounding, video reasoning and STEM benchmarks. | CNJD |
| [2609.14708](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14708) | Lightning Weave: Improving the Accuracy-Efficiency Frontier of Reasoning Models through Capability Composition [code](https://github.com/jet-ai-projects/Lightning-Weave) | General LLM | Multi-teacher | Represents each expert's capability as its policy drift relative to the model it was post-trained from, combines these log-ratio shifts on shared student token states and turns them into a stable distillation target, with the anchor scored only once. Result: For Qwen3.5-4B, HMMT 2025 goes 59.2% → 64.0% and LCB v5 41.7% → 54.2%, with fewer tokens. | N |
| [2609.15404](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15404) | Who Teaches Which Token? Verifier-Gated Multi-Expert On-Policy Distillation for Scientific Reasoning | General LLM | Multi-teacher | Teacher signal is sparse and heterogeneous along a reasoning trajectory: counterfactual gains of experts on specific criteria decide who is qualified to teach, teacher-student disagreement locates which tokens to teach, and the gated KL enters GRPO as an advantage. Result: The 4B and 8B students are best overall on seven benchmarks and each rank first on five of them; mismatching the supervision budget is the change that hurts performance most. | CTD |
| [2609.23377](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23377) | One to More, More to One: Category-Aware Iterative Expert Training for Software Engineering Agents [code](https://github.com/alibaba/AgenticBigBang) | Agents | Multi-teacher | SWE agents train per-category experts and then merge them: each expert alternates long-horizon RL with repair SFT on its own successful trajectories, label-routed multi-teacher OPD integrates them, and gating keeps only improving directions. Result: 86.19% on SWE-bench Pro V2 and 71.78% multilingual, 4.57/3.89pp above the base model. | ND |
| [2609.23697](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23697) | Distill What You Trust: Reliability-Aware Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation [code](https://github.com/sunjie279/TrustMOPD-) | General LLM | Multi-teacher | Turns multi-teacher OPD into a label-free token-level allocation: each expert's preference shift relative to a shared reference model measures local reliability, and after calibration it weights that teacher's distillation loss. Result: Closes 91.5%/98.0% of the teacher-student gap on SingleCap/MultiCap, against only around 54% for the strongest label-free baseline. | CTND |
| [2609.29464](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29464) | TS-OPD: Reconciling ASR and QA in Speech Language Models via Task-Specific On-Policy Distillation | Multimodal understanding | Multi-teacher | Uses the models before and after ASR specialization as the QA teacher and the ASR teacher respectively, with the student sampling trajectories per task and supervised only by the matching teacher, easing signal competition. Result: Improves basic and contextual ASR recognition while keeping QA capability, is robust to the balance coefficient and keeps benefiting as data grows. | CJ |
| [2609.30837](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30837) | MOPD-Router: Rethinking Teacher Routing in Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation [code](https://github.com/TURLEing/MOPD-Router) | General LLM | Multi-teacher | Multi-teacher OPD that routes supervision per token over the whole teacher pool, using ExpertAlign to measure whether a teacher's current correction reflects its expertise, with no domain labels needed. Result: On unlabeled mixed data it beats Mean aggregation by 5.88 points (+12.3%); on labeled data it beats standard MOPD by 3.95 points (+7.8%). | CD |
| [2609.33838](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33838) | ChemOPD: Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for Multi-Task Chemical Reasoning | Other applications | Multi-teacher | Multi-teacher OPD for chemistry: uses task affinities estimated from SFT gradients to solve an MIP that partitions overlapping expert groups, keeps a generalist teacher during distillation, and gradually increases the contribution of routed experts with an anchor-residual objective. Result: Affinity-based grouping beats semantic grouping; a stronger teacher does not mean a stronger student, and anchor-residual OPD is better on most metrics. | D |
| [2609.34605](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34605) | PMOPD: Task Ordering, Cycling, and Parameter-Update Subspace Protection in Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation [code](https://github.com/ant-research/PMOPD) | General LLM | Multi-teacher | Finds that in multi-teacher OPD, parameter updates for different tasks quickly concentrate in their own low-dimensional subspaces; on this basis it uses a subspace memory to project out gradients that interfere with protected directions, and designs task ordering and cycling strategies. Result: The three-task average score is +2.54 on Qwen2.5-7B and +2.09 on Llama-3.1-8B. | D |
| [2609.34658](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34658) | CapField-OPD: Learning Continuous Capability Fields via Joint-Anchored Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for Flow Models | Image, video and audio generation | Multi-teacher | Uses multiple reward-expert teachers as anchors that form a continuous capability field, with explicit capability coordinates deciding the mix of supervision targets, so invoking a capability is no longer tied to prompt semantics and its strength can be adjusted at inference time. Result: Matches or exceeds each expert on compositional generation, text rendering and aesthetics, and supports continuous capability control and coordinate-based test-time scaling. | J |
| [2609.34745](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34745) | No Pain, More Gain: Iterative Merging for Effective Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | Multi-teacher | Points out that student initialization in MOPD strongly affects capability recovery while initial benchmark scores are unreliable, and proposes starting from a uniform merge and progressively adding task-vector increments for under-recovered domains during training. Result: In a 5-domain setting, the average normalized recovery rate is higher than MOPD initialized from a uniform merge or warmed up with SFT. | CD |
| [2609.35347](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35347) | Beyond Teacher Assignment: Domain-Normalized Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation [code](https://github.com/LiXin97/DN-MOPD) | General LLM | Multi-teacher | Finds that in multi-teacher OPD, teacher feedback scales are uneven across domains and the instruction-following signal is too dispersed and dominates updates, so it normalizes and rescales each domain's feedback by its measured dispersion. Result: Average scores beat MOPD across six public benchmarks, three model scales and three random seeds, recovering most of the math gains. | CTD |
| [2610.00333](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00333) | LEGO-OPD: Factorized Teacher Composition for Multimodal On-Policy Distillation | Multimodal understanding | Multi-teacher | Composes a single teacher distribution in a generalized Bayesian way: a language expert gives a prior over candidate tokens while a vision expert contributes only the image-induced likelihood update, with prefix-adaptive calibration of visual supervision strength. Result: On the Qwen3 series, beats the evaluated single- and multi-teacher OPD baselines on both multimodal and text-only reasoning, improving perception while preserving text reasoning. | C |
| [2610.02324](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02324) | Slow-Fast Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for Capability Preservation | Multimodal understanding | Multi-teacher | Uses the student's slow EMA model as a capability reference and, in log-probability space, removes the component of each teacher update that pushes away from the slow model, mitigating capability interference in multi-teacher distillation. Result: Across multiple model scales, reduces average degradation of general capabilities and keeps specialized multimodal capabilities consistently above vanilla MOPD. | CD |
| [2610.02359](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02359) | Lexicographic Multi-Objective On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | Multi-teacher | Selects experts level by level by reward priority: for each student rollout it picks the expert for the first objective that falls short, and projects out correction components conflicting with higher-priority experts. Result: In the four-expert setting, retains about 90% of the accuracy and reasoning-correctness gains, versus only about 57% for the best baseline. | C |
| [2610.02381](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02381) | Latent-MOPD: Latent Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | Multi-teacher | The first representation-level multi-teacher on-policy distillation: beyond output distributions it aligns teacher hidden states, bridging widths with a shared projection, grouping by domain, and shifting gradually from hidden states to tokens. Result: In the same-family setting, outperforms token-only, representation-only, and uniform-averaging baselines on all nine math/code/logic benchmarks. | CD |
| [2610.08398](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08398) | UP-MOPD: Update Projection in Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | Multi-teacher | Handles conflicts in multi-teacher OPD at the optimizer output: the mixed gradient first updates the optimizer state to give a candidate step, and a candidate that violates the constraints is projected onto the feasible update nearest in Euclidean distance. Result: In the medical-plus-general mix, IFEval-loose late in training is 2.96 points above naive M-OPD, with an eight-item average of 60.03. | ND |
| [2610.10460](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10460) | Composing What Each Teacher Learned: Delta Distillation for Multi-Teacher On-Policy Learning [code](https://github.com/HJSang/Delta-MOPD) | General LLM | Multi-teacher | Multi-teacher OPD that transfers not the teachers' endpoint policies but the teacher-minus-base logit shift, re-anchored to the student's frozen initialization, removing the pull term inherited from the base. Result: With three teachers combined, it beats endpoint supervision by 4.11 math points and 1.95 five-benchmark points; staged routing cuts the ordering gap from 10.50 to 6.42. | ND |
| [2610.13521](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13521) | Beyond Domain-Routed Supervision: Cross-Teacher Guarding for Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation [code](https://github.com/jokersio-tsy/RoadMOPD) | General LLM | Multi-teacher | In multi-teacher OPD, non-routed teachers act as cross-domain reviewers on the same student trajectory: teacher agreement drives token-level credit assignment, and auxiliary gradients drive a preventive projection that eases gradient conflicts on shared parameters. Result: In the high-conflict setting the weaker domain improves by more than 5 percentage points, and overall results also improve in the low-conflict and three-domain settings. | D |
| [2610.16172](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16172) | EventLens: Event-Structure Reinforcement Learning for Video Understanding | Multimodal understanding | Multi-teacher | An RL framework for video event structure: verifiable rewards train specialized policies per task (event segmentation, progress discrimination, multi-event relations), then multi-teacher on-policy distillation merges these specialized policies into a unified model. Result: On Qwen3-VL, temporal grounding and event-sensitive reasoning improve while general video QA performance is maintained, at lower training cost than direct mixed RL. | - |
| [2610.20174](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20174) | RM-OPD: On-Policy Distillation for Generative Reward Models | General LLM | Multi-teacher | First trains domain-expert teachers with single-domain GRPO, then merges the multiple teachers into one generative reward model through routed on-policy distillation plus domain-contribution reweighting. Result: Outperforms baselines such as Mix-RL on multiple RM benchmarks and two base models, with a beyond-teacher effect emerging. | - |
| [2610.21234](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21234) | Compatibility Matters: A Framework for Token-Level Teacher Selection in On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | Multi-teacher | Proposes a framework for choosing the teacher token by token during rollout, and argues from teacher-student reasoning-pattern compatibility, teacher competence and student diversity under which conditions different routing schemes apply. Result: On math, code, kernel generation and visual reasoning, pass@k is up to about 10 percentage points higher than vanilla OPD and the teacher experts. | - |
| [2610.21250](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21250) | FocusOPD: Structure-Guided On-Policy Distillation for Diffusion Models | Image, video and audio generation | Multi-teacher | Analysis shows that teacher-student feature mismatch in multi-teacher OPD concentrates in deep Transformer blocks and early denoising steps, so token-level directional feature alignment is added only in that region. Result: Compared with DiffusionOPD, GenEval 0.953→0.967 and PickScore 23.797→23.944. | - |
| [2610.21522](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21522) | Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for Flow-Based Vision-Language-Action Models | Embodied | Multi-teacher | Two-level on-policy distillation for flow-matching VLA: the student collects its own environment interactions and generates action-flow trajectories, and multiple frozen domain experts provide velocity-field supervision along these trajectories. Result: Absolute success rate gains of +22.55% on LIBERO-40, +33.4% on CALVIN and +22.6% on Meta-World MT50. | D |
| [2610.22217](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22217) | Skill-Decoupled On-Policy Expert Distillation for Unified Vision-Language Models [code](https://github.com/NEUIR/SCOPED) | Multimodal understanding | Multi-teacher | Trains experts decoupled by capability and extracts explicit skills via reward-guided reflection; the skills are given to the teacher but not the student, and multi-expert on-policy distillation runs along student trajectories. Result: Consistently beats the backbone model and mixed-capability RL on eight multimodal evaluations. | - |
| ★[2610.22236](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22236) | Training-Free On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | Multi-teacher | Points out that expert merging needs no training: each expert's input second moments when reading its own answers come from a few forward passes, and the merged model is solved layer by layer in closed form by least squares. Result: On Olmo-3-7B, forward computation takes a few GPU hours per expert, and most gains are kept on seven math and code evaluations. | - |
| [2610.22681](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22681) | EgoP²C: Internalizing Plan-Perceive-Compose for Spatial Reasoning in Egocentric Video | Multimodal understanding | Multi-teacher | First trains two teachers, for planning and perception, with dual LoRA, then assigns multi-teacher on-policy distillation to different supervisors by trajectory segment, internalizing the tool-style pipeline into a single VLM. Result: The two backbones improve by 9.2 and 7.5 points on average, respectively, and inference FLOPs drop by 65.8–66.3%. | - |
| [2610.26124](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26124) | Nash-MOPD: Optimizing Task Trade-offs in Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | Multi-teacher | Reuses existing teacher-student reverse KL statistics to build a local minimax game for domain scheduling, giving more prompts to domains that improve slowly and need more samples. Result: By step 100 it already exceeds the final math/code scores of both baselines, and leads on eight of nine metrics. | D |
| [2610.26950](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26950) | Beyond Policy Discrepancy: Capability-Aware Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | Multi-teacher | First merges RL experts into a strong initialization, then uses the verifier-estimated teacher-student capability gap rather than behavioral difference to modulate distillation strength per domain, weakening supervision as the gap converges. Result: Averages 32.22 over six benchmarks, 0.86/0.83/0.74 higher than naive MOPD/Open-MOPD/merged initialization. | D |
| [2610.36973](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36973) | KL-MOPD: Discrepancy-Aware Multi-Objective On-Policy Distillation for Generative Recommendation | Other applications | Multi-teacher | In generative recommendation, two single-objective teachers for click and for conversion are distilled into the student token by token, with teacher weights assigned from the initial token-level teacher-student KL to strengthen supervision from the harder-to-distill teacher. Result: Proved strictly better than plain MOPD in theory, with gains on three public benchmarks, billion-scale industrial data and online A/B. | - |
| [2610.39876](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39876) | Turing LLM Post-Training: Calibrated Distillation and Efficient Dynamic-MoE Execution | General LLM | Multi-teacher | Expert consolidation for dynamically routed MoE: multi-teacher OPD matches the teachers on the local top-k support with teacher-level normalization and clipping, accelerated by asynchronous rollout-training overlap. Result: Four-domain consolidation score of 0.9988; the merging step drops from 398.7 seconds to 139.3 seconds, a 2.86× speedup. | - |

## Combining OPD and RL (34)

| arXiv | Title | Domain | Teacher | What it does | Lists |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [2608.00485](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00485) | SERL-SQL: Selective Hindsight Distillation for Text-to-SQL Reinforcement Agentic Learning [code](https://github.com/Ffunkytao/SERL-SQL) | Agents | Same model, privileged | A multi-turn Text-to-SQL agent samples on-policy trajectories, a teacher re-scores them with execution feedback, and the teacher-student likelihood gap weights GRPO advantages for credit assignment. Result: 76.56% execution accuracy on BIRD-Dev and 89.92% on Spider-Test. | C |
| [2608.01597](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01597) | HindSearch: Trajectory-Level Hindsight Critique for Search-Augmented Reinforcement Learning | Agents | Same model, privileged | In search RL, a frozen judge reads the gold answer and writes a short critique of each failed trajectory; the critique enters GRPO as an auxiliary on-policy distillation signal on the student's search actions. Result: Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct reaches 39.4% average EM on seven benchmarks; removing the gold answer makes the gain largely disappear. | T |
| [2608.07371](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07371) | Trajectory-Relative Hindsight Distillation for Agentic Reinforcement Learning | Agents | Same model, privileged | Sets the supervision direction from the log-probability gap of the same response under the ordinary and the hindsight-conditioned context, and normalizes turn-level quotas over the whole trajectory. Result: On WebShop, Qwen3-1.7B success rises from 56.4% to 75.2%. | CN |
| [2608.11604](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11604) | Learning from Online User Feedback for Shopping Agents | Other applications | Same model, privileged | Learns from real e-commerce logs: RL on verifiable purchase outcomes, while instructive feedback in user conversations is turned into dense token-level supervision. Result: On real logs, recommendation quality, response helpfulness and satisfaction alignment all beat strong baselines. | - |
| [2608.24310](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24310) | OPDSearch+: On-Policy Distillation with RL Refinement for Search-Augmented Reasoning | Agents | External white-box | Search-augmented reasoning without teacher fine-tuning: in stage one the student interacts with a real search engine and is distilled by an off-the-shelf instruct teacher with per-position forward KL, and stage two refines it with RL. Result: The 3B model beats all existing 3B RL baselines, with +13.1% on HotpotQA and +8.5% on 2WikiMultihopQA. | CTN |
| [2608.24696](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24696) | On-policy Distillation with Verifiable Reward [code](https://github.com/LeapLabTHU/OPDVR) | General LLM | External white-box | Rewrites the implicit reward of sampled-token OPD according to trajectory correctness, then uses ReLU gating to make it non-negative on correct trajectories and non-positive on incorrect ones, turning it into an RLVR method with no extra hyperparameters. Result: Consistently beats standard OPD on six reasoning benchmarks. | CTN |
| [2608.31077](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31077) | Reconciling Process Supervision with Outcome-Based Credit in Agentic Policy Optimization | Agents | Same model, privileged | In agentic RL, aggregates the token-level likelihood changes from privileged self-distillation onto executable actions and turns them into bounded, mean-preserving weights that redistribute the trajectory advantage, whose direction and scale are still set by the outcome reward. Result: Improves on GRPO by 10.6% across three agentic benchmarks and generalizes better to unseen tasks. | - |
| [2609.01422](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01422) | From Rollouts to Recipes: Self-Contained Post-Training for LLMs | General LLM | Same model, privileged | Based on the correctness and confidence of each sample's rollouts, routes the sample to GRPO, on-policy self-distillation or regularization, or skips it, with no external teacher or extra sampling. Result: On math reasoning with Qwen3 and Qwen3.5 it reliably beats uniform GRPO, uniform OPSD and a fixed mix. | - |
| [2609.05295](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05295) | RISE: Recursive Improvement via Self-Extrapolating Policy Distillation | General LLM | Own earlier checkpoint | Extrapolating the displacement between the current checkpoint and a lagging anchor along the RLVR trajectory synthesizes a dense token-level teacher that needs no external model or privileged context and is refreshed every round. Result: Outperforms plain RLVR and on-policy self-distillation across math reasoning, multi-domain STEM, code generation and multi-turn agent tasks. | CNH |
| [2609.26708](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26708) | Train Where the Quantized Model Goes: On-Policy Distillation for Low-Bit Reasoning | Quantization and pruning | External white-box | Notes that the exposure bias of quantization-aware distillation is amplified along the model's own trajectory, so the student self-samples through the quantized forward path used at deployment while a full-precision teacher gives dense supervision on its prefixes. Result: Across four models at 2.79/1.88 equivalent bits, the MATH-500 retention rate goes 35% → 70% and HumanEval 66% → 91%. | CN |
| [2609.28385](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28385) | When and Where to Trust the Teacher: Unifying On-Policy Distillation and GRPO through Entropy-Calibrated Credit Assignment | General LLM | External white-box | Unifies verifier and teacher signals within a single GRPO update: the teacher path log-ratio enters the reward before group normalization, then credit is redistributed with entropy calibration from teacher-student token differences and zero sum within a response. Result: Avg@12 of Qwen3-1.7B/4B is 17.21%/65.09%, 0.89/0.56pp above the strongest baseline. | CTN |
| ★[2609.32674](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32674) | Expected Reasoning-Step Return Unifies On-Policy Learning from Rewards and Teachers | General LLM | External white-box | Treats reasoning steps as macro-actions and uses Monte Carlo returns from student rollouts to compare student and teacher actions in the same space, then reinforces the student on successful trajectories and distills the teacher on failed ones. Result: Finds an outcome-dependent asymmetry: student actions are more beneficial on successful trajectories, while teacher replacement is more beneficial on failed ones. | C |
| [2609.33414](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33414) | TTRSD: Test-Time Reinforcement Learning with Self-Distillation for Vision-Language Models | Multimodal understanding | Same model, privileged | Label-free test-time adaptation: the same policy aggregates answer distributions over original/cropped/downsampled views to serve as the teacher, and uses visual ablation to pick visually sensitive token positions to update. Result: With only 20 unlabeled samples, InternVL3-2B's MMMU rises from 35.79% to 49.32%. | - |
| [2609.35058](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35058) | TIDE: Teacher-Student Transition via Informative Distillation and Exploration for Agentic RL | Agents | External white-box | Schedules the global handoff from OPD to RL by the downward trend of teacher-student divergence, and at the turn level jointly uses relative action value and normalized divergence to weight teacher-guided versus reward-driven updates. Result: Multiple benchmarks, multiple student scales and controlled ablations support the effectiveness of its adaptive OPD-RL coordination (the abstract gives no specific numbers). | T |
| [2609.36742](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36742) | SIPO: Unifying Reinforcement Learning with On-Policy Self-Distillation | General LLM | Same model, privileged | To counter self-teacher overconfidence, builds two teacher contexts pairing the reference answer with within-group mistakes, and uses the difference of the two log-probabilities to cancel shared bias and turn it into a token-level advantage. Result: Outperforms RLVR and OPSD baselines on multiple reasoning and code benchmarks; still provides a learning signal when every rollout in a group fails. | CH |
| [2609.37898](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37898) | Guide, Then Let Go: Gap-Adaptive Teacher Scheduling for Sparse-Reward Agentic RL [code](https://github.com/Ricardo-H/guide-then-let-go) | Agents | Weak teacher | In sparse-reward agentic RL, adds a token-level OPD term on the student's rollouts whose weight adaptively decays with the teacher-student performance gap, withdrawing teacher guidance once the student reaches the teacher's reference level. Result: Achieves the highest average success rate across three teacher-student configurations, 4.37%-11.87% above reward-only GRPO. | CT |
| [2609.38854](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38854) | Mitigating the Length-Scaling Tax with Online Distillation | General LLM | Own earlier checkpoint | Proposes a length-scaling tax metric and routes already-solved prompts to on-policy distillation with an exponential moving average of the online policy as teacher, while unsolved prompts continue with the original RL objective. Result: Single-turn reasoning length tax 19.0% to -3.7%, multi-turn agentic tasks 31.4% to 13.7%, with performance on par with or better than RL. | X |
| [2609.39436](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39436) | From Imitation to Reward Discovery: On-Policy Warmup for Agentic RL | Agents | External white-box | Finds that OPD initialization accelerates agentic RLVR, proposes a warmup stage of teacher supervision on the student's own interaction trajectories, and gives theory via reverse KL and trajectory-distribution matching. Result: In the main comparisons, OPD-initialized RLVR reaches high performance earlier, with higher average and final performance than other baselines. | C |
| [2610.00332](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00332) | The Weakest Link: Distilling LLM Reasoning with Worst-Case Constrained Reinforcement Learning | General LLM | External white-box | Casts reasoning distillation as constrained RL: maximize verifiable task reward while imposing a worst-case constraint on the teacher log-likelihood at every prefix of the trajectory, and derives an equivalent reward transformation free of state augmentation. Result: On math and code tasks, expands the accuracy-fidelity Pareto front, matching pure RL's final-answer correctness while greatly reducing teacher constraint violations. | C |
| [2610.00838](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00838) | SHARPO: Segment-Level Credit Assignment for Agentic Reinforcement Learning | Agents | Same model, privileged | Drawing on OPSD, computes teacher-student log-probability gaps within each environment-interaction segment and turns them into a bounded multiplier that scales the GRPO advantage of tokens in that segment. Result: Qwen2.5-7B outperforms GRPO, SDAR, RLSD, and StepOPSD on ALFWorld and WebShop. | - |
| [2610.02781](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02781) | OPD Before RL: Warm-Starting Rubric-Based RL with On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | Same model, privileged | Two-stage training: first uses the rubric as the teacher's privileged context and has the rubric-free student match the teacher distribution on its self-sampled prefixes, then runs RL with rubric rewards. Result: The two-stage recipe is best on three health-science tasks including HealthBench, with less reward hacking than SFT+RL. | C |
| [2610.04596](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04596) | DiffGate: Difficulty-Gated Teacher Guidance for On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | Combines GRPO with selective teacher guidance: teacher supervision is applied only to failed trajectories, scaled by group difficulty with smooth bounded clipping, and the verifier decides who gets the teacher signal. Result: On code, Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B gain +1.7/+1.8 avg@8 and +1.6/+5.7 pass@8 over GRPO. | C |
| [2610.08077](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08077) | Self-Retrospection Distillation: Turning Post-hoc Experiences into Prior Foresight [code](https://github.com/SalesforceAIResearch/SRD) | Agents | Same model, privileged | Distills hindsight knowledge from completed trajectories into the same policy's foresight predictions before interaction, as extra supervision when RLVR rewards are homogeneous and a group has no contrast; no explicit foresight generation is needed at inference. Result: Up to +24.2pp on 10 tool-reasoning and long-horizon agent tasks; at 2B, when 98% of groups fail entirely, RLVR reaches 0.0% while adding SRD reaches 60.6%. | - |
| [2610.11167](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11167) | PIVOT: Perplexity-Informed KD-to-RL Transition Scheduling for Vertical-Domain Few-Shot Distillation | Other applications | External white-box | Routes each sample by teacher-evaluated sequence perplexity: low-perplexity samples move to GRPO for reward-based fine-tuning, while high-perplexity samples stay in OPD to absorb domain knowledge. Result: On Banking77 and HWU64, it consistently beats continued OPD and globally synchronized OPD→GRPO at the same number of post-training steps. | N |
| [2610.11519](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11519) | Residual Advantage: Student-Relative Teacher Guidance for RL with Verifiable Rewards | General LLM | External white-box | Treats the teacher-student probability residual as a bounded one-step reward, subtracts the state value under the student policy to form the advantage and centers it within each response, so credit is only redistributed inside a response. Result: With 1.7B/4B students and an 8B teacher, it wins all 24 comparisons on three math benchmarks, Avg@8 +1.7–3.6, beating pure OPD. | TNK |
| [2610.15772](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15772) | RSIPO: Recursive Self-Improving Policy Optimization for Agentic Reinforcement Learning via Skill-Policy Co-Evolution [code](https://github.com/zfkarl/RSIPO) | Agents | Same model, privileged | A self-evolving agent combining RLVR and OPSD: the policy itself extracts skills from trajectories and optimizes skill generation through replay, then uses empirical Bayes shrinkage to calibrate how much it trusts the teacher's scores. Result: Qwen2.5-7B reaches a 98.6% average success rate on ALFWorld, 2.5% higher than the second-best baseline and 17.4% higher than GRPO. | - |
| [2610.15840](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15840) | LLM Value Functions for Credit Assignment in Agentic RL | Agents | External white-box | Uses a pretrained LLM as a value function without fine-tuning for step-level credit assignment (LVRL), and extends it to LVOPD, which focuses teacher supervision on actions where the estimated value drops for on-policy distillation. Result: Beats GRPO by up to 13.6/8.6 points on AppWorld and SWE-bench Verified, and raises BabyAI from total failure to 75.0%. | - |
| [2610.17953](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17953) | Agent-Reflex: Teaching Language Agents to Act Reflectively [code](https://github.com/RUC-NLPIR/Agent-Reflex) | Agents | Same model, privileged | A three-stage reflection framework: perturbs tool feedback to mine success-failure pairs and summarize reflective meta-skills, first performs reflective SFT, then combines reflection-guided OPD with agentic RL in noisy environments. Result: Qwen3-8B scores 58.6 overall on five long-horizon tool-use benchmarks and 29.1 on AgentNoiseBench, surpassing environment-scaling and reflection baselines. | - |
| [2610.19271](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19271) | Outcome-Routed Distillation and Reinforcement Learning for Diffusion Language Models | Diffusion language models | External white-box | On MoE diffusion language models, routes rollout groups by verification outcome: mixed groups use ratio-free REINFORCE, and all-wrong groups use dense KL from the teacher on the denoising canvas. Result: With 24 denoising steps, distillation beats the teacher by 6.77 points and routing adds a further 18.31 points; math results vary with the seed. | - |
| [2610.22240](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22240) | MMSearch-OPD: Easy Made Hard, Hard Made Learnable for Long-Horizon Multimodal Search Agents | Agents | External white-box | Multimodal search agent: parametrically rewrites easy questions to extend reasoning chains, trains jointly with RL and OPD, and uses the reward gap to modulate the teacher's token-level guidance on imperfect student trajectories, decaying it gradually. | - |
| [2610.23048](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23048) | Distill Until Divergence: Error-Truncated Hindsight Distillation for Agentic RL | Agents | Same model, privileged | Routes two kinds of hindsight skills by trajectory outcome and error boundary: positive skills reinforce successful procedures and pre-error decisions, negative skills target critical errors, and the post-error suffix is left to GRPO alone. | - |
| [2610.23234](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23234) | MultiAgent-OPD: End-to-End Training of Hierarchical Multi-Agent Systems with On-Policy Distillation | Agents | External white-box | End-to-end training of hierarchical multi-agent systems: a frozen teacher scores the search and summary tokens generated by students as local process supervision, while the leader learns from team success and the answerer from candidate correctness. Result: Macro-average avg@4 across seven datasets is 38.68%, 4.06 points above a prompted critic. | - |
| [2610.27694](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27694) | Enhancing Bug-Exposing Test Generation via Step-Level Credit Assignment and Self-Distillation from Valuable Failures [code](https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SCALF-F4D1) | General LLM | Same model, privileged | Process-supervised RL for test generation: it gives each step an independent advantage from the process reward and routes trajectories that trigger the bug but produce wrong output into on-policy self-distillation based on execution feedback. Result: On three benchmarks the joint success rate, output correctness rate and trigger rate are up to 2.97/5.26/3.53 percentage points higher. | - |
| [2610.38566](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38566) | Co-Evolving Policy and Rubric via Capability Awareness for Open-Ended Generation | General LLM | Same model, privileged | Co-evolves the scoring rubric and the policy: it diagnoses rubric difficulty by contrasting rollouts given the question alone with rollouts given the rubric, then either runs guided optimization or uses on-policy self-distillation to internalize the capability that guidance reveals. Result: Stably outperforms rubric-based RL across several benchmarks and models, and mitigates capability degradation. | - |

## Cross-tokenizer and cross-architecture (16)

| arXiv | Title | Domain | Teacher | What it does | Lists |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| ★[2608.03316](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03316) | Any-OPD: Heterogeneous On-Policy Distillation for Flow-Matching Models via Representation-Space Bridging | Image, video and audio generation | Black-box | Flow-matching on-policy distillation across model families: the teacher acts as a black-box sampler, decoded images are compared only in a frozen vision representation space, and alignment follows continuous noise levels. Result: FLUX.1-dev→SD3.5-M: PickScore 0.846→0.884, HPSv3 9.12→10.97. | TJ |
| ★[2608.14277](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14277) | SimpleOPD: Simple Tokenizer-Agnostic On-Policy Distillation for Long-Context Reasoning [code](https://github.com/hhnqqq/SimpleOPD) | General LLM | External white-box | Achieves cross-vocabulary OPD by aligning only span-consistent tokens in a shared text space, and suppresses length explosion with a student-reference KL and a terminator-token advantage mask. Result: Intern-S2 gains +21.2 points on ProofBench to reach 55.2, surpassing Gemini-2.5-Pro. | CT |
| [2608.21229](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21229) | Beyond Attention Masks: Instruction Anchoring for Efficient In-Context Diffusion Generation | Image, video and audio generation | External white-box | Reorders the token layout with static text anchors so reference KV can be reused across denoising steps, then performs architectural quality recovery with teacher supervision on the states the student visits. Result: Matches full-attention quality on image, speech and video generation, and speeds up DiT inference by up to 6.40×. | TJ |
| [2608.27857](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27857) | SpikeOPD: Stable On-Policy Distillation for Autoregressive Spiking Language Models | Quantization and pruning | External white-box | On-policy distillation from an ANN teacher to a spiking-network student: full KL correction on self-generated prefixes, plus prefix-matched policy anchoring and layer-wise spike regularization to suppress rollout feedback collapse. Result: At the three scales of 0.125B/0.35B/1.3B, average accuracy is 0.8/1.7/2.9 points higher than the corresponding KD-version SNN. | CN |
| [2609.10154](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10154) | CompassOPD: Cross-Family On-Policy Distillation via Within-Family Likelihood Shifts | General LLM | External white-box | Finds that cross-family OPD fails even with aligned tokenizers, splits the signal into cross-family shift and within-family likelihood shift, transfers only the latter, and anchors updates with a frozen student reference. Result: Average reasoning accuracy improves by up to 5.50 points across three student families, and a MoE teacher without a reference checkpoint still gives a 3.43-point gain. | CNK |
| [2609.31947](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31947) | On-Policy Attention Linearization | Quantization and pruning | External white-box | A hybrid linear-attention student samples its own long-context trajectories under dense supervision from a frozen full-attention teacher, addressing the fact that off-policy distillation does not teach the model to recover from state drift. Result: With only 3B training tokens it recovers 87–94% on commonsense reasoning, 100% on NIAH retrieval and 83–93% on math, without SFT or RLVR. | - |
| [2609.32448](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32448) | ForkLeft: Entropy-First Rollouts for Prefix-Aligned Autoregressive-to-Diffusion Distillation | Diffusion language models | External white-box | A diffusion language model first runs entropy-first rollouts to expose fork points, then fixes that student prefix and lets an autoregressive teacher supervise in the same context; inference still uses native parallel decoding. Result: Efficient-DLM-4B improves on all ten benchmarks, with MATH500 rising from 72.60% to 79.60%. | C |
| [2609.33816](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33816) | Dual-Vocabulary Language Model for Cross-Tokenizer Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | Cross-vocabulary OPD: equips the teacher with a student-vocabulary projection head to get full-dimensional student logits, and supports student token inputs with parallel token sequences and mixed-prefix attention. Result: The DVLM teacher converges to a loss close to the original teacher's, and the student improves on six reasoning tasks. | C |
| [2609.34371](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34371) | From Static to Dynamic: On-Policy Distillation from Image to Video Diffusion Models | Image, video and audio generation | External white-box | Uses cheaper image experts as teachers to supervise along the video student's own generated trajectories: a learnable linear connector aligns the heterogeneous latent spaces, and a motion constraint and an optical-flow reward are added to keep temporal consistency. Result: Consistently beats video-teacher OPD baselines across multiple image experts and video student backbones. | CJ |
| [2609.34738](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34738) | Beyond Token Alignment: Event Completion for Cross-Tokenizer On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | In cross-tokenizer OPD, when the student generates only a prefix of a teacher token, supervises the total probability of the set of byte-compatible one-step completions instead of split probabilities. Result: Consistent gains in math/code/science reasoning, scaling to distilling a 1T teacher into a 35B student; one-step completions cover over 99% of compatible teacher mass. | - |
| [2609.35362](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35362) | d-OPD: Future-Aware On-Policy Distillation for Block Diffusion Language Models [code](https://github.com/mit-han-lab/d-OPD) | Diffusion language models | External white-box | When going from an AR teacher to a block diffusion student, the two condition on inconsistent information; d-OPD folds the future information visible within a block into the teacher distribution as a correction, so supervision matches the state the student actually sees. Result: On Qwen3 0.6B~8B, the six-benchmark average is up to +4.0 points over OPDLM, and training time is cut by 1.35~1.58×. | C |
| [2610.08448](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08448) | Rethinking Cross-Tokenizer On-Policy Distillation: From Alignment Coverage to Supervision Reliability | General LLM | External white-box | Examines whether wider alignment coverage helps cross-tokenizer OPD: strict 1:1 alignment already covers most tokens, and reverse KL over the student-selected top-16 at strict positions alone is enough. Result: Adding MSE supervision on mismatched spans actually lowers scores: its gradients are weakly correlated with, or even opposite in direction to, strict-position gradients, and larger in magnitude. | TNK |
| [2610.13257](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13257) | CAST: Credit Alignment for Cross-Tokenizer On-Policy Distillation | General LLM | External white-box | Cross-tokenizer OPD: distributes each chunk's advantage to student tokens by uncertainty and teacher-signal smoothness, with KL anchoring where tokens are unaligned. Result: On six benchmarks, cross-vocabulary transfer gains +2.45 and +1.32 points on average, and same-vocabulary 1.7B gains +2.10 points. | - |
| [2610.17397](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17397) | JustOPD: On-Policy Distillation from Autoregressive to Diffusion Language Models | Diffusion language models | External white-box | OPD from an autoregressive teacher to a diffusion language model student: during training, student rollouts are generated autoregressively to match the teacher's left-to-right decoding, while inference keeps the DLM's flexible generation order. Result: With a Qwen3-14B teacher and a Dream-7B student, math reasoning beats the base DLM under various divergence choices. | - |
| [2610.36466](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36466) | Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion-Uni: Elastic Self-Refining Unified Diffusion LLM with Cross-Paradigm On-Policy Distillation [code](https://github.com/NVlabs/Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion-Uni) | Diffusion language models | Multi-teacher | Cross-paradigm on-policy distillation transfers the understanding of an autoregressive VLM and the generation of a continuous flow model into a unified discrete diffusion LLM, with a progressively self-refining diffusion process. Result: Best among unified DLLMs on visual question answering, multimodal reasoning, object grounding, text-to-image generation and image editing. | - |
| [2610.42128](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42128) | DecisionRank: On-Policy Distillation for Decision-Model Rerankers | Other applications | External white-box | Treats the list ranked by the student's scores as a rollout (OPD in item space), with a generative teacher giving conditional feedback on that ranking to directly supervise the discriminative reranker's scores. Result: The 1.7B student beats a 27B generative teacher on TREC DL19-23; the 8B is 24.5× faster than generative listwise reranking. | - |

## Related Work (136)

Papers that meet only part of the OPD definition, or use OPD as just one stage of a training pipeline (such as technical reports). Grouped by domain.

### Technical reports (35)

| [2608.06756](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06756) | Capek 0.5: An Execution-Centric Vision-Language Model for Embodied Intelligence | Technical reports | Multi-teacher | An embodied VLM technical report: four capability experts are each trained with RLVR, then combined into a single model through weight merging and routed policy-space distillation. Result: Two sizes, 2B and 35B-A3B, with a single checkpoint retaining all four kinds of expert capability. | N |
| ★[2608.09119](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09119) | Motif 3: Technical Report | Technical reports | Multi-teacher | Technical report on a 314B MoE with 13.2B activated: post-training merges six RL expert teachers and one SFT teacher into a unified model with multi-teacher on-policy distillation. Result: Competitive with leading open-source models on evaluations such as long-horizon agentic tasks, math reasoning and scientific knowledge. | T |
| [2608.13505](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13505) | Intern-S2-Preview: Scientific Agentic Foundation Model | Technical reports | - | Technical report on a scientific agentic foundation model whose post-training includes stages such as SFT, multi-task RL, agentic RL and on-policy distillation. Result: Live-Stream QA 94.8 (base 80.3), with P50/P95 latency of 3.4s/8.1s on a single H20. | TNK |
| [2608.16386](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16386) | Mint-Agent: Introducing Finance-Native Agentic Foundation Models | Technical reports | Multi-teacher | Technical report on financial agents: SFT + key-step OPD + RLVR trains reasoning and execution experts, which are then unified into a general model with model merging and multi-teacher on-policy distillation. Result: RFC-Bench 98.33% (Mint-Ag), FinSearchComp T2 69.86% (Mint-Cu). | T |
| [2608.20334](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20334) | Exploring the Performance Frontier of Compact Unified Image Generation Models | Technical reports | Multi-teacher | Technical report on a 6B unified image generation and editing model; post-training uses parallel expert RL plus multi-teacher on-policy distillation to reduce interference between heterogeneous objectives, and OPD is only one step of the pipeline. Result: At 6B and 243K GPU training hours it reaches leading overall performance among open-source models, and compressing it to 3B is nearly lossless. | J |
| [2609.00791](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00791) | Instella-MoE Technical Report | Technical reports | Multi-teacher | Technical report on a fully open-source 16B MoE model; at the end of the post-training pipeline, multi-teacher on-policy distillation is used for reinforcement learning, and OPD is just one step in the whole pipeline. Result: Average of 76.7 on pre-training benchmarks, and a post-training average of 73.2 for the Think version. | TK |
| [2609.08183](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08183) | NeoHorse-1: Towards Recursive Self-Improvement via Agentic Post-Training with Routing Harness [code](https://github.com/TokenRhythm/NeoHorse) | Technical reports | External white-box | Technical report for an agent model: routing logs are turned into training samples that organize a three-stage SFT curriculum, extended to routing-guided on-policy distillation that closes the self-improvement loop. Result: Across eleven benchmarks the macro average rises from 58.94 to 64.87 for 4B and from 65.60 to 69.04 for 9B. | K |
| [2609.08368](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08368) | Miles v0.1: Production-Level Post-Training [code](https://github.com/radixark/miles) | Technical reports | - | Technical report for a production-level post-training system: an SGLang rollout engine with two training backends and three weight synchronization modes, supporting on-policy distillation in addition to full-parameter RL. Result: Fully asynchronous agentic RL on GLM-5.2 744B-A40B over 64 GB300 cards, with a median step time of 263 seconds over the first 30 steps. | NK |
| [2609.11127](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11127) | KuaiRP Series Role-playing Models Technical Report | Technical reports | Same model, privileged | Technical report for role-playing models: after SFT and rule-reward RL, two-stage OPD with cumulative divergence decay uses the domain model as teacher and the base model as student to restore general capability. Result: Matches SOTA closed-source models on in-domain role-playing fidelity while restoring general agentic capability and keeping deployment cost low. | NK |
| [2609.11929](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11929) | SenseNova-U1.5: Towards Native Unified Visual Intelligence [code](https://github.com/OpenSenseNova/SenseNova-U1) | Technical reports | Multi-teacher | Technical report for an 8B natively unified multimodal model: post-training separately optimizes experts for visual aesthetics, bilingual text rendering, infographic generation and image editing, then merges their capabilities with multi-expert on-policy distillation. Result: Large gains in image fidelity, text rendering, complex composition and multi-reference editing, with the training code including OPD to be open-sourced. | JDK |
| [2609.18310](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18310) | SEA-LION-v4.8: A Technical Report | Technical reports | External white-box | Technical report for a Southeast Asian language model family built on NVIDIA Nemotron 3: continued pretraining on Southeast Asian language, reasoning, code and parallel corpora, then post-training with SFT plus online on-policy distillation. Result: On SEA-HELM, 30B-A3B rises from 46.06 to 51.57 and 120B-A12B from 49.30 to 63.44. | NK |
| [2609.24526](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24526) | ME-VLM: A Unified VLM for Embodied Cognition and Agent Coordination [code](https://github.com/MachEmbodied/ME-VLM) | Technical reports | Multi-teacher | A report on a VLM unifying embodied cognition and multimodal agents: two kinds of experts are trained separately with RL, merged into a single model with multi-teacher on-policy distillation, and deployed on device with quantization. Result: On-device prefill latency of the 4B variant drops from 400ms to 188ms, with competitive results on embodied and agent benchmarks. | TJD |
| [2609.25176](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25176) | Qwen-Audio-3.1-Realtime: Towards Reliable Agentic Voice Interaction | Technical reports | Multi-teacher | A report on a real-time voice agent: the Think stage uses multimodal multi-teacher OPD to transfer language capability and build native audio skills, while the Act stage uses GRPO in an executable environment. Result: Task success on the half-duplex adaptation of τ-Voice goes 78.4% → 82.0%, and the false response rate to background speech goes 73.0% → 13.0%. | J |
| [2609.26375](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26375) | KwaiMind Technical Report | Technical reports | Multi-teacher | A report on an e-commerce image editing system: specialized policies trained on rewards such as CTR, text rendering and product consistency are finally merged into one editing model with on-policy distillation. Result: The share whose predicted CTR beats the original product image goes 12.16% → 37.41%, and an online A/B test gives about a 2.44% relative gain in actual CTR. | J |
| [2609.28554](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28554) | Pistis Technical Report | Technical reports | External white-box | Technical report for a multimodal large model: it proposes IDRL, interleaved distillation and reinforcement learning, which alternates on-policy distillation and RL inside the same training loop rather than using a static joint loss. Result: Both the 27B and 9B scales and both the Thinking and Agentic variants beat their respective base models. | TJ |
| [2609.29626](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29626) | iCoder-27B: Recursive AI-Led Development of Frontier Industrial Coding Model | Technical reports | Same model, privileged | Agents autonomously evolve the data and orchestrate SFT, on-policy self-distillation and RLVR to train a 27B industrial coding model for RTL and GPU kernels. | [2609.37712](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37712) | PolyOCR-Venus: Unified OCR Foundation Models for Text-Centric Visual Intelligence [code](https://github.com/inclusionAI/PolyOCR-Venus) | Technical reports | External white-box | Technical report on unified OCR foundation models whose training combines verifier-based GRPO with on-policy distillation routed per sample by teacher reliability and the teacher-student competence gap. | [2610.01352](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01352) | MMVistaReason: Toward Open-Data and Post-Training Recipes for Multimodal Reasoning [code](https://github.com/JackieForest/MMVistaReason) | Technical reports | Multi-teacher | An open-data multimodal post-training recipe: cascaded teacher distillation builds the SFT/RL data, then complementary RL experts are trained first and consolidated via multi-teacher OPD (MOPD). | [2610.08967](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08967) | Socio-Foundation: A Model for Generalizable Individual Behavior Simulation via Hierarchical Capability Distillation | Technical reports | Multi-teacher | A foundation model for individual behavior simulation: DAPO trains task experts, off-policy distillation turns them into capability experts, and multi-teacher on-policy distillation finally unifies them; OPD is only the last stage of a three-stage pipeline. Result: Gains 11.0 points over the Qwen3-8B base and approaches frontier-model level. | N |
| [2610.11451](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11451) | SAIL: Scientific Agentic Intelligence via a Science-Aware Loop | Technical reports | Multi-teacher | Technical report on a scientific research agent model with 35B total/3B active parameters; training includes SFT, expert training, multi-teacher on-policy distillation and agentic RL, with OPD only one stage of the pipeline. Result: Achieves competitive performance on scientific research tasks with far fewer parameters than mainstream open-weight models. | NK |
| [2610.11959](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11959) | MiMo-V2.6: Scaling Reinforcement Learning Towards Self-Improvement | Technical reports | Multi-teacher | Xiaomi's technical report on scaling omni-modal RL for MiMo-V2.6; the abstract does not mention OPD, but the main text uses MOPD2 (multi-prefix multi-teacher OPD) after mixed RL to merge domain teachers (per the thinkwee and kissshhot collections). | TK |
| [2610.12957](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12957) | JoyInside-VLM-Agent: Product-Conditioned Multimodal Policy Learning for Embodied Agents | Technical reports | Multi-teacher | Technical report on an embodied multimodal agent: RL follows SFT and multi-teacher on-policy distillation, and DPER routes token-level evidence between a frozen privileged teacher and a privileged student. Result: Its BFCL v4 overall score of 77.44 beats the compared baselines, with mixed results on other public benchmarks; it leads across the board on in-house device and robot sets. | - |
| [2610.16550](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16550) | CT0: Cybersecurity Transformer | Technical reports | External white-box | Technical report on a cybersecurity-specialized model of about 4B parameters: the backbone is specialized on in-house CAI data through two-stage on-policy distillation, and the model provides a generation-free fast verdict path and confidence gating. | [2610.22196](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22196) | Hy-Memory-30B-A3B: Attributed Reinforcement Learning for Long-Term Memory Writing | Technical reports | - | A long-term memory writing model: memory on-policy distillation on student-generated states first teaches the write protocols of three frameworks, followed by memory RL with attribution rewards. | [2610.27993](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27993) | Building Telecom Language Models with RSOD: Domain Data, Specialist Post-Training, Memory-Conscious Distillation, and Networking Evaluation | Technical reports | Multi-teacher | An end-to-end report on a telecom domain model: it builds 210 thousand routed training examples to train six LoRA expert teachers, then merges them into a single student adapter with routed on-policy distillation. | [2610.31292](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31292) | Spark-Audio 1.0 Technical Report | Technical reports | - | A speech foundation model trained on domestically produced compute, whose post-training combines supervised finetuning with on-policy distillation as a standard stage. Result: With far less audio data than the compared models it still matches them on most tasks, and is better at multilingual recognition and spoken dialogue. | - |
| [2610.32492](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32492) | Accordion: Elastic-Depth Inference via Fixed-Prefix Layer Looping | Technical reports | Multi-teacher | An MoE with fixed-prefix layer looping supports five inference budgets, merging three domain experts into one model via parameter-group merging and budget-matched multi-teacher on-policy distillation. Result: After 3.6T pretraining, deeper inference raises the macro average over 37 tasks by 3.01 percentage points, and the middle budget captures 89% of the gain with half the extra layer executions. | - |
| [2610.33066](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33066) | OvisOCR3 Technical Report [code](https://huggingface.co/ATH-MaaS/OvisOCR3) | Technical reports | Multi-teacher | A 0.8B unified OCR model trained with multi-teacher on-policy distillation from five 4B domain teachers, followed by reinforcement learning with mixed task and domain rewards. | [2610.36013](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36013) | OmniBrain-0: A Unified Agentic Model for Embodied Intelligence [code](https://public-bots.github.io/OmniBrain-0/) | Technical reports | Multi-teacher | A unified embodied decision model that first distills manipulation and navigation experts, then merges them with multi-teacher on-policy distillation, and refreshes the experts through rule-guided recursive self-improvement. | ★[2610.38095](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38095) | Nemotron 3.5 Super and Lightning: Open, Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Hybrid Mamba-Transformer Models for Agentic AI | Technical reports | Multi-teacher | A report on open MoE hybrid models: beyond SFT and RL, Super's post-training uses specialized teacher training plus multi-teacher on-policy distillation to merge multimodal and agentic capabilities. | [2610.39301](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39301) | Youtu-Parsing-Omni: One Encoder, One Schema, All Modalities | Technical reports | Same model, privileged | An omni-modal parsing model whose post-training uses OSAD to align, on the model's own rollouts, with the token-level targets given by a reference-conditioned copy of itself, refreshing the teacher with the trained weights each round.
